Content: Upon receiving indication from the MCPTT client that encoded voice is received from the user or if encoded voice is already buffered the floor participant: shall request the MCPTT client to start forward encoded voice to the MCPTT server; and shall remain in the 'U: has permission' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'U: has permission' > Send Floor Release message (PTT button released)
Content: Upon receiving an indication from the user to release the permission to send RTP media, the floor participant: shall send a Floor Release message towards the floor control server. The Floor Release message: may include the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Release message set to '1' (acknowledgement is required) as specified in clause 8.2.2; NOTE: It is an implementation option to handle the receipt of the Floor Ack message and what action to take if the Floor Ack message is not received. void; and if the Floor Granted message included the G-bit set to '1' (Dual floor), shall include the Floor Indicator with the G-bit set to '1' (Dual floor); shall remove the indication that the participant is overriding without revoke if this indication is stored; shall remove the indication that the participant is overridden without revoke if this indication is stored; shall start timer T100 (Floor Release) and initialize counter C100 (Floor Release) to 1; and shall enter the 'U: pending Release' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'U: has permission' > Receive Floor Revoke message (R: Floor Revoke)
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Revoke message, the floor participant: shall inform the user that the permission to send RTP media is being revoked; may give information to the user about the reason for revoking the permission to send media; shall request the media mixer in the MCPTT client discard any remaining buffered RTP media packets and to stop forwarding encoded voice to the MCPTT server; if the G-bit in the Floor Indicator is set to '1' (Dual floor): shall send a Floor Release message. In the Floor Release message: shall include the Floor Indicator with the G-bit set to '1' (Dual floor); and may set the first bit in the subtype to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2; if the G-bit in the Floor Indicator is set to '0' (not Dual floor): shall send a Floor Release message. In the Floor Release message: shall include the Floor Indicator with the G-bit set to '0' (not Dual floor); and may set the first bit in the subtype to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2; NOTE: It is an implementation option to handle the receipt of the Floor Ack message and what action to take if the Floor Ack message is not received. shall start timer T100 (Floor Release) and initialize counter C100 (Floor Release) to 1; and shall enter the 'U: pending Release' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'U: has permission' > Receive Floor Granted message (R: Floor Granted)
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Granted message from the floor control server, the floor participant: if the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Granted message is set to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2, shall send a Floor Ack message. The Floor Ack message: shall include the Message Type field set to '1' (Floor Granted); and shall include the Source field set to '0' (the floor participant is the source); and shall remain in the 'U: has permission' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'U: has permission' > Receive RTP media packets (R: RTP Media)
Content: Upon receiving RTP media packets the floor participant: 1. shall request the MCPTT client, based on configuration, either to continue rendering or stop the rendering and start storing the received RTP media packets if an indication is stored that the participant is overriding without revoke; NOTE: The configuration whether to continue rendering or to start storing the incoming RTP media is out of the scope of the present document. shall request the MCPTT client to render the received RTP media packets if an indication is stored that the participant is overridden without revoke; and shall remain in the 'U: has permission' state. NOTE: RTP media packets can be received from multiple sources when dual floor control is applied by the floor control server (see clause 6.3.6) or when multi-talker control is applied by the floor control server. The MCPTT client can differentiate between the different sources using the SSRC in the received RTP media packets. How the media mixer in the MCPTT client mixes the different RTP media stream sources is out of scope of the present document.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'U: has permission' > Receive Floor Idle message (R: Floor Idle)
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Idle message from the floor control server, the floor participant: if an indication that the participant is overriding without revoke is stored: if the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Idle message is set to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2, shall send a Floor Ack message. The Floor Ack message: shall include the Message Type field set to '5' (Floor Idle); and shall include the Source field set to '0' (the floor participant is the source); shall remove the indication that the participant is overriding without revoke; and shall remain in the 'U: has permission' state; and if the G-bit in the Floor Indicator is set to '1' (Dual Floor) and an indication that the participant is overridden without revoke is stored: if the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Idle message is set to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2, shall send a Floor Ack message. The Floor Ack message: shall include the Message Type field set to '5' (Floor Idle); and shall include the Source field set to '0' (the floor participant is the source); shall remove the indication that the participant is overridden without revoke; and shall remain in the 'U: has permission' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'U: has permission' > Receive Floor Taken message (R: Floor Taken)
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Taken message: if the G-bit in the Floor Indicator set to '1' (Dual Floor) the floor participant: shall inform the user that the call is overridden without revoke; shall store an indication that the participant is overridden without revoke; and shall remain in the 'U: has permission' state; and if the I-bit in the Floor Indicator set to '1' (Multi-talker) the floor participant: may provide a floor taken notification to the user; should start the optional timer T103 (End of RTP media) for the participant for which Floor Taken message was received; shall provide a notification to the user indicating the type of call and may provide a list of current talkers; and shall remain in the 'U: has permission' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'U: has permission' > Receive Floor Release Multi Talker message (R: Floor Release Multi talker)
Content: Upon receiving the Floor Release Multi Talker message, the floor participant: shall provide a notification to the user indicating that the participant has released the floor in a multi-talker group; and shall remain in the 'U: has permission' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'U: pending Release' > General
Content: The floor participant is in this state when the floor participant is waiting for response to a Floor Release message. In this state the floor participant can receive floor control messages and RTP media packets. Timer T100 (Floor Release) is running in this state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'U: pending Release' > Timer T100 (Floor Release) expired
Content: On expiry of timer T100 (Floor Release) less than the configurable number of the upper limit of counter C100 (Floor Release) times, the floor participant: shall send a Floor Release message towards the floor control server; may set the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Release message to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2; NOTE: It is an implementation option to handle the receipt of the Floor Ack message and what action to take if the Floor Ack message is not received. shall restart timer T100 (Floor Release) and increment counter C100 (Floor Release) by 1; and shall remain in state 'U: pending Release'.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'U: pending Release' > Timer T100 (Floor release) expired N times
Content: When timer T100 (Floor Release) expires by the upper limit of counter C100 (Floor Release) times, the floor participant: 1. shall enter the 'U: has no permission' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'U: pending Release' > Receive Floor Idle message (R: Floor Idle)
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Idle message, the floor participant: if the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Idle message to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2, shall send a Floor Ack message. The Floor Ack message: shall include the Message Type field set to '5' (Floor Idle); and shall include the Source field set to '0' (the floor participant is the source); may provide a floor idle notification to the MCPTT user; if the Floor Indicator field is included and the type of call bit is set, may provide a notification to the user indicating the type of call; shall stop timer T100 (Floor Release); if the session is not a broadcast group call or if the A-bit in the Floor Indicator field is set to '1' (Normal call), shall enter the 'U: has no permission' state; and if the session was initiated as a broadcast group call: shall indicate to the MCPTT client the media transmission is completed; and shall enter the 'Releasing' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'U: pending Release' > Receive Floor Taken message (R: Floor Taken)
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Taken message, the floor participant: if the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Taken message is set to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2, shall send a Floor Ack message. The Floor Ack message: shall include the Message Type field set to '2' (Floor Taken); and shall include the Source field set to '0' (the floor participant is the source); may provide floor taken notification to the user; 3. if the Floor Indicator field is included and the type of call bit is set, may provide a notification to the user indicating the type of call; if the Floor Indicator field is included and the I-bit is set to '1' (multi-talker), shall provide a notification to the user indicating the type of call and may provide a list of current talkers; may store the Granted Party's Identity and may display the identity of the talking party to the user; shall store the Audio SSRC of the Granted Participant, to be able to associate received RTP media packets with this communication in case of multiplexing; should start the optional timer T103 (End of RTP media) for each new talker as received in Floor Taken message; if the identity of the floor participant is not included in the List of Granted Users, shall stop timer T100 (Floor Release); and shall enter the 'U: has no permission' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'U: pending Release' > Receive RTP media packets (R: RTP Media)
Content: Upon receiving an indication from the MCPTT client that RTP media packets are received, the floor participant: shall request the MCPTT client to start rendering the RTP media packets; should start the optional timer T103 (End of RTP media) for the participant from which the RTP packets were received; void; and shall remain in the 'U: pending Release' state. NOTE: RTP media packets can be received from multiple sources when dual floor control is applied by the floor control server (see clause 6.3.6) or when multi-talker control is applied by the floor control server. The MCPTT client can differentiate between the different sources using the SSRC in the received RTP media packets. How the media mixer in the MCPTT client mixes the different RTP media stream sources is out of scope of the present document.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'U: pending Release' > Receive Floor Revoke message (R: Floor Revoke)
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Revoke message, the floor participant: may give information to the user that permission to send RTP media is being revoked; may inform the user of the reason contained in the Floor Revoke message; and shall remain in the 'U: pending Release' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'U: pending Release' > Receive Floor Granted message (R: Floor Granted)
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Granted message, the floor participant: if the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Granted message is set to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2, shall send a Floor Ack message. The Floor Ack message: shall include the Message Type field set to '1' (Floor Granted); and shall include the Source field set to '0' (the floor participant is the source); and shall remain in the 'U: pending Release' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'U: pending Release' > Receive Floor Release Multi Talker message (R: Floor Release Multi talker)
Content: Upon receiving the Floor Release Multi Talker message, the floor participant: shall provide a notification to the user indicating that the participant has released the floor in a multi-talker group; and if the message is due to the participant having released the floor shall enter 'U: has no permission', otherwise shall remain in the 'U: pending Release' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > In any state > General
Content: This clause describes the actions to be taken in all states defined for the basic state diagram with the exception of the 'Start-stop' state and the 'Releasing' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > In any state > Receive MCPTT call release step 1 (R: MCPTT call release - 1)
Content: Upon receiving an MCPTT call release step 1 request from the application and signalling plane when the MCPTT call is going to be released or when the floor participant is leaving the MCPTT call, the floor participant: shall stop sending floor control messages and stop all running timers; shall request the MCPTT client to stop sending RTP media packets; and shall enter the 'Releasing' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > In any state > Send Queued Floor Requests message (S: Send Queued Floor Requests)
Content: Upon receipt of a request from an MCPTT user to cancel the floor requests of other MCPTT users whose floor requests queued or a request from an MCPTT user to clear all the users floor requests queued by the floor control server for supporting queuing, if the group is not configured for audio cut-in floor control, the floor participant: shall send the Queued Floor Requests message as described in clause 8.2.15 including a Queued Floor Requests Purpose field with the Queued Floor Requests Purpose value set to '0' (Cancel Request) if: the user provided a list of MCPTT users, then include the list of MCPTT users in the List of Queued Users field; or the user did not provide a list of MCPTT users, then the List of Queued Users field is not included; (This is coding indicates that the user is requesting that all floor requests queued by the floor control server for supporting queuing); shall start timer T134 (Queued Floor Requests); and shall remain in the current state.NOTE: It is an implementation option to handle the receipt of the Floor Ack message and what action to take if the Floor Ack message is not received. [NOTE:]It is an implementation option to handle the receipt of the Floor Ack message and what action to take if the Floor Ack message is not received. If the group is configured for audio cut-in floor control, the floor participant shall indicate to the MCPTT user that request to cancel the queued floor requests not allowed on the group configured for audio cut-in floor control.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > In any state > Timer T134 (Queued Floor Requests) expired
Content: On expiry of timer T134 (Queued Floor Requests), the floor participant: shall provide a Queued Floor Requests timeout to the MCPTT client, and shall remain in the current state. NOTE: It is an implementation option to handle the timer expiry event and what action to take.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > In any state > Receive Queued Floor Requests message (R: Queued Floor Requests)
Content: Upon receiving a Queued Floor Requests message, including a Queued Floor Requests Purpose field with the Queued Floor Requests Purpose value set to '1' (Cancel Result), the floor participant: if the first bit in the subtype of the Queued Floor Requests message is set to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2, shall send a Floor Ack message. The Floor Ack message: shall include the Message Type field set to '14' (Queued Floor Requests); and shall include the Source field set to '0' (the floor participant is the source); may provide the result of a message for cancellation of a queued floor request to the MCPTT user; shall stop the timer T134 (Queued Floor Requests), if running; and shall remain in the current state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'Releasing' > General
Content: The floor participant is in this state while waiting for the application and signalling plane to finalize the disconnection of an MCPTT call.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'Releasing' > Receive MCPTT call release step 2 (R: MCPTT call release - 2)
Content: Upon receiving an MCPTT call release step 2 request from the application and signalling, the floor participant: shall release all resources including any running timers associated with the MCPTT call; and shall enter the 'Start-stop' state and terminate the current instance of the 'Floor control state machine basic'.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'U: queued' > General
Content: The floor participant uses this state when a Floor Request message has been queued by the floor control server, and is awaiting the Floor Granted message. In this state, the MCPTT client can receive RTP Media packets and the floor participant can send and receive floor control messages. The timer T104 (Floor Queue Position Request) can be running in this state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'U: queued' > Receive RTP media packets (R: RTP media)
Content: Upon receiving an indication from the media mixer in the MCPTT client that the media mixer is receiving RTP media packets, the floor participant: shall request to the media mixer to start rendering received RTP media packets; should restart timer T103 (End of RTP media) from which RTP packets were received; and shall remain in the 'U: queued' state. NOTE: RTP media packets can be received from multiple sources when dual floor control is applied by the floor control server (see clause 6.3.6) or when multi-talker control is applied by the floor control server. The MCPTT client can differentiate between the different sources using the SSRC in the received RTP media packets. How the media mixer in the MCPTT client mixes the different RTP media stream sources is out of scope of the present document.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'U: queued' > Receive Floor Taken message (R: Floor Taken)
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Taken message, the floor participant: may provide a floor taken notification to the MCPTT user; if the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Taken message is set to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2, shall send a Floor Ack message. The Floor Ack message: shall include the Message Type field set to '2' (Floor Taken); and shall include the Source field set to '0' (the floor participant is the source); if the Floor Indicator field is included and the I-bit is set to '1' (multi-talker), shall provide a notification to the user indicating the type of call and may provide a list of current talkers; may store the Granted Party's Identity and may display the identity of the talking party to the user; shall store the Audio SSRC of the Granted Participant, to be able to associate received RTP media packets with this communication in case of multiplexing; should start the optional timer T103 (End of RTP media); and shall remain in the 'U: queued' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'U: queued' > Receive Floor Granted message (R: Floor Granted)
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Granted message, the floor participant: if the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Granted message is set to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2, shall send a Floor Ack message. The Floor Ack message: shall include the Message Type field set to '1' (Floor Granted); and shall include the Source field set to '0' (the floor participant is the source); shall provide a floor granted notification to the MCPTT user; if the Floor Indicator field is included and the type of call bit is set, may provide a notification to the user indicating the type of call; shall stop timer T104 (Floor Queue Position Request), if running; shall start timer T132 (Queued granted user action); shall stop the optional timer T103 (End of RTP media), if running, and if associated to a participant for whichthe previously received Floor Taken did not include a Floor Indicator field with the G-bit set to '1' (Dual floor); shall indicate the user that the floor is granted; shall store the Audio SSRC of the Granted Participant and use it in the RTP media packets until the floor is released; and shall remain in the 'U: queued' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'U: queued' > Receive Floor Deny message (R: Floor Deny)
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Deny message, the floor participant: if the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Deny message is set to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2, shall send a Floor Ack message. The Floor Ack message: shall include the Message Type field set to '3' (Floor Deny); and shall include the Source field set to '0' (the floor participant is the source); shall provide floor deny notification to the MCPTT user; may display the deny reason to the user using information in the Reject Cause field; shall stop timer T104 (Floor Queue Position Request), if running; and shall enter the 'U: has no permission' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'U: queued' > Send Floor Release message (PTT button released)
Content: Upon receiving an indication from the MCPTT user to release the queued floor request, the floor participant: shall send a Floor Release message: The Floor Release message: void; may set the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Release message to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2; NOTE: It is an implementation option to handle the receipt of the Floor Ack message and what action to take if the Floor Ack message is not received. shall start timer T100 (Floor Release) and initialise counter C100 (Floor Release) to 1; shall stop timer T104 (Floor Queue Position Request), if running; shall stop timer T132 (queued request granted user action); and shall enter the 'U: pending Release' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'U: queued' > Receive Floor Queue Position Info message (R: Floor Queue Position Info)
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Queue Position Info message, the floor participant: if the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Queue Position Info message is set to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2, shall send a Floor Ack message. The Floor Ack message: shall include the Message Type field set to '9' (Floor Queue Position Info); and shall include the Source field set to '0' (the floor participant is the source); if the message indicates that the request has been queued or if a request for the queue position was sent, the floor participant: may provide the queue position and priority (if available) to the MCPTT user; shall stop the timer T104 (Floor Queue Position Request), if running; and shall remain in the 'U: queued' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'U: queued' > Receive Floor Idle message (R: Floor Idle)
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Idle message, the floor participant: if the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Idle message is set to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2, shall send a Floor Ack message. The Floor Ack message: shall include the Message Type field set to '5' (Floor Idle); and shall include the Source field set to '0' (the floor participant is the source); may provide a floor idle notification to the MCPTT user; shall stop timer T104 (Floor Queue Position Request), if running; and shall enter the 'U: has no permission' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'U: queued' > Send Floor Queue Position Request message (S: Floor Queue Position Request)
Content: Upon receipt of an indication from the MCPTT client to request the queue position and timer T132 is not running (i.e. a Floor Granted message has not been received), the floor participant: shall send the Floor Queue Position Request message; shall start timer T104 (Floor Queue Position Request) and initialize counter C104 (Floor Queue Position Request) to 1; and shall remain in the 'U: queued' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'U: queued' > Timer T104 (Floor Queue Position Request) expired
Content: On expiry of timer T104 (Floor Queue Position Request) less than the upper limit of C104 (Floor Queue Position Request) times, the floor participant: shall send a Floor Queue Position Request message towards the floor control server; shall restart timer T104 (Floor Queue Position Request) and increment counter C104 (Floor Queue Position Request) by 1; and shall remain in the 'U: queued' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'U: queued' > Timer T104 (Floor Queue Position Request) expired N times
Content: When timer T104 (Floor Queue Position Request) expires by the upper limit of counter C104 (Floor Queue Position Request) times, the floor participant: shall provide a floor queued timeout to the MCPTT client; may set the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Release message to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2; shall send the Floor Release message; NOTE: It is an implementation option to handle the receipt of the Floor Ack message and what action to take if the Floor Ack message is not received. shall start timer T100 (Floor Release) and initialise counter C100 (Floor Release) to 1; and shall enter the 'U: pending Release' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'U: queued' > User indication for accept of pending request
Content: Upon receiving an indication from the user that the user wants to send media and the timer T132 (Queued granted user action) is running, the floor participant: shall stop timer T132 (queued request granted user action); and shall enter 'U: has permission' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'U: queued' > Timer T132 (Queued granted user action) expires
Content: Upon expiry of timer T132 (Queued granted user action) the floor participant: shall send Floor Release message; may indicate the user that the floor is no more available; may set the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Release message to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2; and NOTE: It is an implementation option to handle the receipt of the Floor Ack message and what action to take if the Floor Ack message is not received. 4. shall enter 'U: has no permission' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'U: queued' > Receive Floor Release Multi Talker message (R: Floor Release Multi talker)
Content: Upon receiving the Floor Release Multi Talker message, the floor participant: shall provide a notification to the user indicating that a participant has released the floor in a multi-talker group; and shall remain in the 'U: queued' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation > State: 'U: queued' > Receive Queued Floor Requests message (R: Queued Floor Requests)
Content: Upon receiving a Queued Floor Requests message including a Queued Floor Requests Purpose field with the Queued Floor Requests Purpose value set to '2' (Cancel Notification), the floor participant: if the first bit in the subtype of the Queued Floor Requests message is set to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2, shall send a Floor Ack message. The Floor Ack message: shall include the Message Type field set to '14' (Queued Floor Requests); and shall include the Source field set to '0' (the floor participant is the source); shall provide a queued floor requests cancellation notification to the MCPTT user; may display the requesting user for a cancellation of a queued floor request to the user using information in the Requested Party's Identity field; shall stop timer T104 (Floor Queue Position Request), if running; and shall enter the 'U: has no permission' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > General
Content: The floor control server arbitration logic in the floor control server shall support the procedures in clauses 6.3.2 and 6.3.3 and shall behave according to the floor control server state transition diagram for 'general floor control operation' in clause 6.3.4. The floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server shall behave according to the floor control server state transition diagram for 'basic floor control operation towards the floor participant' as specified in clause 6.3.5.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Controlling MCPTT function procedures at MCPTT call initialization > General
Content: The clause 6.3.2.2 describes the initial procedures when a new SIP session is establishing a group session or a private session with floor control. The clause 6.3.2.3 describes the procedures when a non-controlling MCPTT function switches from the non-controlling mode to the controlling mode.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Controlling MCPTT function procedures at MCPTT call initialization > Initial procedures
Content: When an MCPTT call is established a new instance of the floor control server state machine for 'general floor control operation' is created. For each MCPTT client added to the MCPTT call, a new instance of the floor control server state machine for 'basic floor control operation towards the floor participant' is added. If the optional "mc_queueing" feature is supported and has been negotiated as specified in clause 14, the floor control server could queue the implicit floor control request for the media-floor control entity. The initial SIP INVITE request or SIP REFER request to establish an MCPTT chat group call or to rejoin an ongoing MCPTT call is not handled as an implicit floor control request message by the floor control server unless explicitly stated in the SIP INVITE request or in the SIP REFER request. The permission to send media to the inviting MCPTT client due to implicit floor control request is applicable to both confirmed indication and unconfirmed indication. When the first unconfirmed indication is received from the invited participating MCPTT function (see 3GPP TS 24.379 ) the floor control server optionally can give an early indication to send RTP media packets, to the inviting MCPTT client. If an early indication to send RTP media packets is given to the inviting MCPTT client, the floor participant is granted the permission to send media and the MCPTT server buffers RTP media packets received from the MCPTT client at least until the first invited MCPTT client accepts the invitation or until the RTP media packet buffer maximum limit is exceeded. If the MCPTT server does not support or does not allow media buffering then an early indication to send RTP media packets is not given to the inviting MCPTT client, the floor participant is granted the permission to send media when the first invited MCPTT client accepts the media. Before the floor control server sends the first floor control message in the MCPTT call, the floor control server has to assign itself a SSRC identifier to be included in media floor control messages and quality feedback messages if the MCPTT server is supporting that option. A suitable algorithm to generate the SSRC identifier is described in IETF RFC 3550 . The floor participant and the floor control server can negotiate the maximum priority level that the floor participant is permitted to request. The floor control server can pre-empt the current sender based on the negotiated maximum priority level that the floor participant is permitted to request and the priority level included in the Floor Request message. NOTE: The maximum priority level that a floor participant can use is negotiated as specified in clause 14.3.3 and is based on group configuration data retrieved by the controlling MCPTT function from the group management server as described in 3GPP TS 24.481 and service configuration data retrieved by the controlling MCPTT function from the configuration management server as described in 3GPP TS 24.484 . For groups configured for audio cut-in floor control, pre-empting of the current sender is not according to priority. Each new floor request results in the floor being revoked from the current talker and being granted to the new requesting talker. The floor participant and the floor control server can negotiate queueing of floor requests using the "mc_queueing" fmtp attribute as described in clause 14. If queueing is supported and negotiated, the floor control server queues the floor control request if a Floor Request message is received when another floor participant has the floor and the priority of the current speaker is the same or higher. Queueing is not permitted for groups configured for audio cut-in floor control.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Controlling MCPTT function procedures at MCPTT call initialization > Switching from a non-controlling MCPTT function mode to a controlling MCPTT function mode
Content: When the MCPTT server switches from the non-controlling MCPTT function mode to controlling MCPTT function mode a new instance of the floor control server state machine for 'general floor control operation' is created. For each MCPTT client in the MCPTT call a new instance of the floor control server state machine for 'basic floor control operation towards the floor participant' is added. Any floor request in the passive floor request queue is moved to the active floor request queue. NOTE: The passive floor request queue is a floor request queue used by the non-controlling MCPTT function as specified in clause 6.5.4 to monitor floor request sent by floor participants controlled by the non-controlling MCPTT function.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > MCPTT floor control procedures at MCPTT call release
Content: When an MCPTT client leaves an MCPTT call and the MCPTT call remains ongoing with the other MCPTT clients, the floor control server follows a two-step procedure. [Step 1]The MCPTT server stops sending floor control messages and RTP media packets to the MCPTT client leaving the MCPTT call and, the MCPTT server discards floor control messages and RTP media packets received from the MCPTT client leaving the MCPTT call. [Step 2]When the application and signalling plane has determined that the MCPTT call with this floor participant has been released, the corresponding instance of the floor control server state machine for 'basic floor control operation towards the floor participant' is released. When an MCPTT call is released, the floor control server follows a two-step procedure. [Step 1]The MCPTT server stops sending floor control messages and RTP media packets to all floor participants in the MCPTT call. [Step 2]When the application and signalling plane has determined that the MCPTT call has been released, the corresponding instance of the floor control server state machine for 'general floor control operation' are also terminated, along with any 'basic floor control operation towards the floor participant' state machines for the floor participants of this call. The floor control server state machine for general floor control operation initiates the MCPTT call release depending on the release policy specified in 3GPP TS 24.379 .
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for general floor control operation > General
Content: The floor control server arbitration logic in the floor control server shall behave according to the state diagram and state transitions specified in this clause. Figure 6.3.4.1-1 shows the general floor control operation states (G states) and the state transition diagram. The floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server shall keep one instance of the 'general floor control operation' state machine per MCPTT call. If floor control messages or RTP media packets arrives in a state where there is no procedure specified in the following clauses the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: shall discard the floor control message; shall request the media distributor in the MCPTT server to discard any received RTP media packet; and shall remain in the current state. State details are explained in the following clauses.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for general floor control operation > State: 'Start-stop' > General
Content: When a new instance of the 'general floor control operation' state machine is initiated, before any floor control related input is applied, the state machine is in 'Start-stop' state. Similarly when the call is released the state machine shall return to the 'Start-stop' state or the related MCPTT call is released.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for general floor control operation > State: 'Start-stop' > MCPTT call initialization
Content: When an MCPTT call is initiated as specified in 3GPP TS 24.379 and if a confirmed indication is required and at least one invited MCPTT client has accepted the invitation; if a confirmed indication is not required; or if the initialised MCPTT call is a temporary group session; NOTE: A MCPTT group call is a temporary group session when the on-network-temporary element is present in the list-service element as specified in 3GPP TS 24.481 . then the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: shall create an instance of the 'general floor control operation' state machine; shall wait for the 'basic floor control operation towards the floor participant' to be initialized before continuing the following steps; when the 'basic floor control operation towards the floor participant' state machine is initialized and the initialised session is not a temporary group session: if the "mc_granted" fmtp attribute is not negotiated as specified in clause 14 or this is an ambient listening call: if the floor control server is granting an implicit floor request at MCPTT call establishment, shall act as if a Floor Request message was received and perform the actions specified in clause 6.3.4.3.3; or if the floor control server is not granting an implicit floor request at MCPTT call establishment, shall enter the'G: Floor Idle' state as specified in clause 6.3.4.3.2; or if the "mc_granted" fmtp attribute is negotiated as specified in clause 14 and this is not an ambient listening call, shall enter the 'G: Floor Taken' state as specified in clause 6.3.4.4.2; and if the 'basic floor control operation towards the floor participant' state machine is initialized and the initialised session is a temporary group session, shall enter the 'G: Initialising' state as specified in the clause 6.3.4.8.1.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for general floor control operation > State: 'G: Floor Idle' > General
Content: The floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server is in this state when no MCPTT user currently has permission to send media. Timer T4 (Inactivity) and timer T7 (Floor Idle) can be running when the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server is in this state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for general floor control operation > State: 'G: Floor Idle' > Enter the 'G: Floor Idle' state
Content: When entering this state from any state except the 'Start-stop' state and if no MCPTT client negotiated support of queueing floor requests as described in clause 14, and the state machine specified in clause 6.3.6 does not exist, the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: if there is a Track Info field associated with the floor control server state transition diagram for 'general floor control operation' stored, shall remove the Track Info field from the storage; if the active floor request queue is empty the floor control server: shall send Floor Idle message to all floor participants. The Floor Idle message: shall include a Message Sequence Number field with a Message Sequence Number value increased with 1; and if a group call is a broadcast group call, a system call, an emergency call, an imminent peril call, or a temporary group session, shall include the Floor Indicator field with appropriate indications; shall start timer T7 (Floor Idle) and initialise counter C7 (Floor Idle) to 1; shall start timer T4 (Inactivity); and shall set the general state to the 'G: Floor Idle' state; and if the active floor request queue is not empty the floor control server: shall select a queued floor request from the top of the active floor request queue; shall remove that queued floor request from the active floor request queue; if the queued floor request includes a Track Info field, shall store the Track Info field and associate it with the floor control server state transition diagram for 'general floor control operation'; and shall enter the 'G: Floor Taken' state as specified in the clause 6.3.4.4.2 with respect to that floor participant. When entering this state from any state except the 'Start-stop' state and the state machine specified in clause 6.3.6 exists, the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: if there is a Track Info field associated with the floor control server state transition diagram for 'general floor control operation' stored, shall remove the Track Info field from the storage; shall send Floor Idle message to all floor participants which are configured to listen to the overridden participant. The Floor Idle message: shall include a Message Sequence Number field with a Message Sequence Number value increased with 1; and if a group call is a broadcast group call, a system call, an emergency call, an imminent peril call, or a temporary group session, shall include the Floor Indicator field with appropriate indications; shall send Floor Taken message to floor participants which are configured to listen only to the overridden participant. The Floor Taken message: if privacy is not requested, shall include the granted MCPTT user's (overriding participant) MCPTT ID in the Granted Party's Identity field; shall include a Message Sequence Number field with a Message Sequence Number value increased with 1; if a group call is a broadcast group call, a system call, an emergency call, an imminent peril call, or a temporary group session, shall include the Floor Indicator field with appropriate indications; shall include the Audio SSRC that was generated for the overridden participant into the Audio SSRC of Granted Participant field; and shall include the location of the user as specified in clause 6.2.4.3.5; shall set the general state to the 'G: Floor Taken' state; and shall send the termination instruction to the 'dual floor control operation' state machine.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for general floor control operation > State: 'G: Floor Idle' > Receive Floor Request message (R: Floor Request)
Content: Upon receiving a floor request message (from a floor participant that is permitted to make a floor request) the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: shall reject the request if one of the following conditions is fulfilled: if there is only one MCPTT client in the MCPTT call; and on-network-recvonly element is present in the entry element as specified 3GPP TS 24.481 for the associated floor participant; if the floor request is rejected the floor control server: shall send the Floor Deny message. The Floor Deny message: shall include in the Reject Cause field the Reject Cause value: cause \#3 (Only one participant), if there is only one MCPTT client in the MCPTT call; or cause \#5 (Receive only), if the on-network-recvonly element is present in the entry element as specified in 3GPP TS 24.481 for the associated floor participant; may include an additional text string explaining the reason for rejecting the floor request in the Reject Phrase value of the Reject Cause field; and if the Floor Request included a Track Info field, shall include the received Track Info field; shall remain in the 'G: Floor Idle' state; and if the floor request is granted the floor control server: shall stop timer T4 (Inactivity); shall stop timer T7 (Floor Idle); shall store the SSRC of floor participant granted the permission to send media until the floor is released associated to that floor request; if a Track Info field is included in the Floor Request message, shall store the received Track Info field, and shall enter the 'G: Floor Taken' state as specified in the clause 6.3.4.4.2.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for general floor control operation > State: 'G: Floor Idle' > Timer T7 (Floor Idle) expired
Content: On expiry of timer T7 (Floor Idle) the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: shall restart timer T7 (Floor Idle) and increment counter C7 (Floor Idle) by 1 if counter C7 (Floor Idle) has not reached its upper limit; shall send a Floor Idle message to all floor participants in the MCPTT call if counter C7 (Floor Idle) has not reached its upper limit. The Floor Idle message: shall include a Message Sequence Number field with a Message Sequence Number value increased with 1; and shall remain in the 'G: Floor Idle' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for general floor control operation > State: 'G: Floor Idle' > Timer T4 (Inactivity) expired
Content: On expiry of timer T4 (Inactivity) the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server based on a configurable service provider policy either: shall indicate to the application and signalling plane that timer T4 (Inactivity) has expired; if the application and signalling planes initiates MCPTT call release, shall enter the 'Releasing' state; and if the application and signalling planes do not initiate MCPTT call release: should restart the T4 (Inactivity) timer; and shall remain in the 'G: Floor Idle' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for general floor control operation > State: 'G: Floor Idle' > Receive an implicit floor request (R: Implicit floor request)
Content: Upon receiving an implicit floor request due to an upgrade to an emergency group call or due to an upgrade to imminent peril call, the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: shall reject the request if there is only one MCPTT client in the MCPTT call; if the floor request is rejected the floor control server: shall send the Floor Deny message. The Floor Deny message: shall include in the Reject Cause field the Reject Cause value cause \#3 (Only one participant); and may include in the Reject Cause field an additional text string explaining the reason for rejecting the floor request in the Reject Phrase value; and shall remain in the 'G: Floor Idle' state; and if the floor request is granted the floor control server: shall stop the timer T4 (Inactivity); shall stop the timer T7 (Floor Idle); shall store the SSRC of floor participant granted the permission to send media until the floor is released associated to that floor request; and shall enter the 'G: Floor Taken' state as specified in the clause 6.3.4.4.2.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for general floor control operation > State: 'G: Floor Idle' > Receive a unicast media stop request (R: Unicast Media Flow Control)
Content: Upon receiving a Unicast Media Flow Control message from a floor participant with Media Flow Control Indicator is set to '0', the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: may de-allocate associated bearer resources by the MCPTT server; shall notify the media distributor to stop sending media to the MCPTT client ; and shall remain in the 'G: Floor Idle' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for general floor control operation > State: 'G: Floor Idle' > Receive a unicast media resume request (R: Unicast Media Flow Control)
Content: Upon receiving a Unicast Media Flow Control message from a floor participant with Media Flow Control Indicator is set to '1', the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: may allocate new bearer resources; shall notify the media distributor to start sending media to the MCPTT client ; and shall remain in the 'G: Floor Idle' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for general floor control operation > State: 'G: Floor Taken' > General
Content: The floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server uses this state when it has permitted one or more of the MCPTT clients in the MCPTT call to send media. Timer T1 (End of RTP media) is running when the floor control server is in this state. If configured to support multi-talker floor control, one instance of timer T1 (End of RTP media) is running per talker that is granted the floor. Timer T2 (End talking) can be running when the floor control server is in this state. If configured to support multi-talker floor control, one instance of timer T20 (Floor Granted) is running per talker that is granted the floor. Timer T20 (Floor Granted) is running to guarantee reliable delivery of the Floor Granted message, if the granted floor request was queued. If configured to support multi-talker floor control, one instance of timer T20 (Floor Granted) is running per talker that is granted the floor. If configured to support multi-talker, then the floor control arbitration logic maintains a list of MCPTT IDs of the currently granted talkers.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for general floor control operation > State: 'G: Floor Taken' > Enter the 'G: Floor Taken' state
Content: When entering this state the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: shall send a Floor Granted message to the floor participant to which the floor is granted. The Floor Granted message: shall include the value of timer T2 (Stop talking)in the Duration field; shall include the granted priority in the Floor priority field; if a Track Info field associated with the floor control server state transition diagram for 'general floor control operation' is stored, shall include the stored Track Info field; if a group call is a broadcast group call, system call, emergency call, an imminent peril call or a temporary group session, shall include the Floor Indicator field with appropriate indications; if the call is a remotely initiated ambient listening call, shall set the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Granted message to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2 ; and f . shall include in the Audio SSRC of Granted Participant field a globally unique audio SSRC, generated and stored by the floor control server, to be used by the granted MCPTT participant in the RTP media packets it will send; [NOTE:]If the call is an ambient listening call and the ambient listening call type is remote-initiated, then the floor participant to which the floor is granted is the terminating floor participant of the call. Otherwise the floor is granted to the participant which requested the floor. shall start timer T20 (Floor Granted) if the floor request was queued for the participant to which the floor is granted and initialise the counter C20 (Floor Granted) to 1; shall send Floor Taken message to all other floor participants. The Floor Taken message: if the floor is currently granted only to one particpant: shall include the granted MCPTT user's MCPTT ID in the Granted Party's Identity field, if privacy is not requested; may include the functional alias of the granted MCPTT user in the Functional Alias field, if privacy is not requested; and shall include the location of the user as specified in clause 6.2.4.3.5; if multi-talker is supported and the floor is currently granted to multiple participants: i . shall include the Floor Indicator field with the I -bit set to '1' ( Multi-talker ); shall include the list of granted users in the multi-talker group in List of Granted Users field, including a new granted talker; shall include the list of Audio SSRCs of Granted Participants; may include the list of functional aliases of the granted floor participants in the List of Functional Aliases field; and shall include the List of Locations of granted floor participants; shall include a Message Sequence Number field with a Message Sequence Number value increased with 1; if the session is a broadcast group call or an ambient listening call, shall include the Permission to Request the Floor field set to '0'; if the session is not a broadcast group call, may include the Permission to Request the Floor field set to '1'; if a group call is a broadcast group call, a system call, an emergency call, an imminent peril call, or a temporary group session, shall include the Floor Indicator field with appropriate indications; and shall include the generated audio SSRC to be used by the granted MCPTT participant into the Audio SSRC of Granted Participant field; shall start timer T1 (End of RTP media) for the participant to which the floor is granted; shall set the general state to 'G: Floor Taken' state; and if configured to support multi-talker floor control the group is configured to shall add the MCPTT identity of the participant to which the floor is granted to the list of currently granted talkers.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for general floor control operation > State: 'G: Floor Taken' > Timer T1 (End of RTP media) expired
Content: On expiry of timer T1 (End of RTP media), the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: shall stop the timer T2 (Stop talking) for the participant to which the floor is granted; if running; shall stop timer T20 (Granted re-send) for the participant to which the floor is granted, if running; shall request the media distributor in the MCPTT server to stop distributing RTP media packets received from the participant for which T1 (End of RTP media) has expired (with the exception of RTP media packets already in the buffer (if RTP media buffering is ongoing)) to other MCPTT clients; if mutli-talker floor control is not supported; shall enter the 'G: Floor Idle' state as specified in the clause 6.3.4.3.2; and if multi-talker floor control is supported, shall send a Floor Release Multi T alker message to all participants other than the one releasing floor. The Floor Release Multi Talker message: shall include SSRC field set to the SSRC of the floor participant for which T1 expired; and shall include t he User ID set to the MCPTT ID of the floor participant for which T1 expired; and shall remove the participant for which timer T1 expired from the list of currently granted talkers, and: if the list of currently granted talkers is empty, shall enter the 'G: Floor Idle' state as specified in the clause 6.3.4.3.2; and if the list of currently granted talkers is not empty shall stay in state the 'G: Floor Taken'.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for general floor control operation > State: 'G: Floor Taken' > Timer T2 (Stop talking) expired
Content: On expiry of timer T2 (Stop talking), the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: shall stop timer T1 (End of RTP media) for the participant for which timer T2 has expired; shall include the Reject Cause field with the Reject Cause value set to \#2 (Media burst too long) in the Floor Revoke message sent in clause 6.3.4.5.2; and shall enter the 'G: pending Floor Revoke' state as specified in the clause 6.3.4.5.2.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for general floor control operation > State: 'G: Floor Taken' > Receive RTP media packets (R: RTP media)
Content: Upon receiving an indication from the media distributor in the MCPTT server that RTP media packets are received from the permitted MCPTT client, the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: if the session is not an ambient listening call, shall start timer T2 (Stop talking) for the participant from which the RTP packet has been received, if not running; shall restart timer T1 (End of RTP media) for the participant from which the RTP packet has been received; shall stop timer T20 (Floor Granted) for the participant from which the RTP packet has been received, if running; shall instruct the media distributor to forward the RTP media packets to MCPTT clients according to local policy; and NOTE: If dual floor control is ongoing as described in clause 6.3.6, the list of floor participants that receive the overriding, overridden, or both transmissions is based on configuration. 5. shall remain in the 'G: Floor Taken' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for general floor control operation > State: 'G: Floor Taken' > Receive Floor Release message (R: Floor Release)
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Release message the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: shall request the media distributor in the MCPTT server to stop forwarding RTP media packets received from the participant that sent the Floor Release message; shall stop timer T2 (Stop talking) for the participant that sent the Floor Release message, if running; shall stop timer T20 (Granted re-send) for the participant that sent the Floor Release message, if running; if mutli-talker floor control is not supported shall enter the 'G: Floor Idle' state as specified in the clause 6.3.4.3.2; and if multi-talker floor control is supported, then: shall send a Floor Release Multi T alker message. The Floor Release Multi Talker message: shall include SSRC field set to the SSRC of the floor participant that has released the floor; and shall include t he User ID set to the MCPTT ID of the floor participant releasing the floor; shall remove the participant that sent the Floor Release message from the list of currently granted talkers; and if the list of currently granted talkers is empty, shall enter the 'G: Floor Idle' state as specified in the clause 6.3.4.3.2; or if the list of currently granted talkers is not empty shall stay in state the 'G: Floor Taken'.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for general floor control operation > State: 'G: Floor Taken' > Receive Floor Request message with pre-emptive priority (R: pre-emptive Floor Request)
Content: NOTE 1: This procedure is also invoked from the clause 6.3.5.4.4. If the group is not configured to support multi-talker feature control, on receipt of a floor request message with effective priority indicating pre-emptive priority, and if the effective priority of the floor participant with permission to send media is not the pre-emptive priority, or if the group is configured for audio cut-in, the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: based on local policy, select one of the following options: revoke the current speaker; or allow media from both the current speaker and from the participant now requesting floor with a pre-emptive floor priority; [NOTE 2:]If the group is configured for audio cut-in, media is allowed only for the participant now requesting the floor. if revoking current speaker is selected: shall stop timer T1 (End of RTP media), if running; shall stop timer T20 (Floor Granted), if running; shall include a Reject Cause field with the Reject Cause value set to \#4 (Media Burst pre-empted) in the Floor Revoke message sent in clause 6.3.4.5.2; shall enter the 'G: pending Floor Revoke' state as specified in the clause 6.3.4.5.2; shall insert the floor participant into the active floor request queue to the position in front of all queued requests, if not inserted yet or update the position of the floor participant in the active floor request queue to the position in front of all other queued requests, if already inserted; and shall send a Floor Queue Position Info message to the requesting floor participant, if negotiated support of queueing of floor requests as specified in clause 14. The Floor Queue Position Info message: include the queue position and floor priority in the Queue Info field; and if the Floor Request message included a Track Info field, shall include the received Track Info field; and if allow media from both the current speaker and from the participant now requesting floor with a pre-emptive priority is selected: shall perform the actions specified in the clause 6.3.6.2.2 6.3.4.4.7a Receive Floor Request message multi-talker (R: multi-talker Floor Request) On receipt of a floor request message and if the group is configured as multi-talker group the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: shall select one of the following options: if the maximum number of simultaneous talkers applicable for multi-talker control is reached and if the floor request message has effective priority indicating pre-emptive priority, determine from all participants having permission to send media, the one with the lowest priority and revoke the floor from the participant with the lowest priority; or if the maximum number of simultaneous talkers applicable for multi-talker control is not reached, allow media from both the current speaker (s) and from the participant now requesting floor; if revoking is selected: shall stop timer T1 (End of RTP media) for the participant from which the floor is revoked, if running; shall stop timer T20 (Floor Granted) for the participant from which the floor is revoked, if running; shall include a Reject Cause field with the Reject Cause value set to \#4 (Media Burst pre-empted) in the Floor Revoke message sent in clause 6.3.4.5.2; shall enter the 'G: pending Floor Revoke' state as specified in the clause 6.3.4.5.2; shall insert the floor participant into the active floor request queue to the position in front of all queued requests, if not inserted yet or update the position of the floor participant in the active floor request queue to the position in front of all other queued requests, if already inserted; shall send a Floor Queue Position Info message to the requesting floor participant, if negotiated support of queueing of floor requests as specified in clause 14. The Floor Queue Position Info message: shall include the queue position and floor priority in the Queue Info field; and if the Floor Request message included a Track Info field, shall include the received Track Info field; and if allow media from both the current speaker(s) and from the participant now requesting floor is selected: shall send a Floor Granted message to the requesting floor participant. The Floor Granted message: shall include the value of the T2 (Stop talking) timer in the Duration field; shall include the granted priority in the Floor priority field; if a Track Info field associated with the floor control server state transition diagram for ' multi-talker floor control operation' is stored, shall include the stored Track Info field; shall include the Floor Indicator field with the I-bit set to '1' (Multi-talker); and shall include in the Audio SSRC of Granted Participant field a globally unique audio SSRC, generated and stored by the floor control server, to be used by the granted MCPTT participant in the RTP media packets it will send; shall add the MCPTT ID of the user to which the floor is granted to the list of currently granted talkers; shall send a Floor Taken message to any non-controlling MCPTT functions involved and to floor participants controlled by the controlling MCPTT function that will listen to the RTP media from the multi-talker MCPTT client according to local policy. The Floor Taken message: shall include the granted MCPTT user's MCPTT ID in the Granted Party's Identity field and may include the associated functional alias in the Functional Alias field, if privacy is not requested; shall include a Message Sequence Number field with a Message Sequence Number value increased with 1; shall include the Floor Indicator field with the I -bit set to '1' ( Multi-talker ); shall include the list of granted users in the multi-talker group in List of Granted Users field; shall include the list of Audio SSRCs of Granted Particpants; and may include the list of functional aliases of the granted floor participants in the List of Functional Aliases field. shall start the T1 (End of RTP media) timer for the participant to which the floor is granted ; shall start timer T20 (Floor Granted) for the participant to which the floor is granted , if the floor request was queued and initialise the counter C20 (Floor Granted) to 1; and shall stay in the state to ' G : Floor Taken' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for general floor control operation > State: 'G: Floor Taken' > Receive Floor request message from permitted floor participant (R: Floor Request)
Content: Upon receiving a floor request message from the floor participant that has been granted permission to send media, the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: shall send a Floor Granted message to the previously granted floor participant. The Floor Granted message: shall include the value of timer T2 (Stop talking) running for this floor participant in the Duration field; shall include the granted priority in the Floor priority field; and if the Floor Request message included a Track Info field, shall include the received Track Info field; and shall remain in the 'G: Floor Taken' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for general floor control operation > State: 'G: Floor Taken' > Timer T20 (Floor Granted) expired
Content: On expiry of timer T20 (Floor Granted), the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: shall send a Floor Granted message to the granted floor participant if counter C20 (Floor Granted) has not reached its upper limit: The Floor Granted message: shall include the value of timer T2 (Stop talking) in the Duration field; shall include the granted priority in the Floor priority field; and if a group call is a broadcast group call, a system call, an emergency call, an imminent peril call, or a temporary group session, shall include the Floor Indicator field with appropriate indications; shall start timer T20 (Floor Granted) and increment counter C20 (Floor Granted) by 1 if counter C20 (Floor Granted) has not reached its upper limit; and shall remain in the 'G: Floor Taken' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for general floor control operation > State: 'G: Floor Taken' > Timer T20 (Floor Granted) expired N times
Content: When timer T20 (Floor Granted) expires and counter C20 (Floor Granted) reaches its upper limit, the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: 1. shall remain in the 'G: Floor Taken' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for general floor control operation > State: 'G: Floor Taken' > Permitted MCPTT client release (R: client release)
Content: If the floor control server receives an indication from the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client that the MCPTT client has started to disconnect from the MCPTT call, the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: if multi-talker floor control is not supported shall enter the 'G: Floor Idle' state as specified in the clause 6.3.4.3.2; and if multi-talker floor control is supported, shall send a Floor Release Multi T alker message. The Floor Release Multi Talker message: shall include SSRC field set to the SSRC of the floor participant that has released the floor; and shall include t he User ID set to the MCPTT ID of the floor participant releasing the floor; and shall remove the participant which is disconnecting from the floor from the list of currently granted talkers, and: if the list of currently granted talkers is empty, shall enter the 'G: Floor Idle' state as specified in the clause 6.3.4.3.2; and if the list of currently granted talkers is not empty shall stay in state the 'G: Floor Taken'.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for general floor control operation > State: 'G: Floor Taken' > Receive an implicit floor request (R: Implicit floor request)
Content: Upon receiving an implicit floor request due to an upgrade to an emergency group call or due to an upgrade to imminent peril call, the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: shall stop timer T1 (End of RTP media), if running; shall stop timer T20 (Floor Granted), if running; shall set the Reject Cause field in the Floor Revoke message to \#4 (Media Burst pre-empted); shall enter the 'G: pending Floor Revoke' state as specified in the clause 6.3.4.5.2; shall insert the floor participant into the active floor request queue to the position in front of all queued requests, if not inserted yet or update the position of the floor participant in the active floor request queue to the position in front of all other queued requests, if already inserted; and shall send a Floor Queue Position Info message to the requesting floor participant, if negotiated support of queueing floor requests as specified in clause 14. The Floor Queue Position Request message: shall include the queue position and floor priority in the Queue Info field; and if a group call is a broadcast group call, a system call, an emergency call, an imminent peril call, or a temporary group session, shall include the Floor Indicator field with appropriate indications.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for general floor control operation > State: 'G: Floor Taken' > Receive Queued Floor Requests message (R: Queued Floor Requests)
Content: Upon receiving a Queued Floor Requests message, including a Queued Floor Requests Purpose field with the Queued Floor Requests Purpose value set to '0' (Cancel Request), from the associated floor participant, the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: if the active floor request queue is empty: shall send a Queued Floor Requests message to the associated floor participant as described in clause 8.2.15. The Queued Floor Requests message: shall include a Queued Floor Requests Purpose field with the Queued Floor Requests Purpose value set to '1' (Cancel Result); shall include a Queued Floor Requests Result field with Queued Floor Requests Result Value set to '2' (The floor request queue is already empty); and if the Floor Request included a Track Info field, shall include the received Track Info field; and may set the first bit in the subtype of the Queued Floor Request s message to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2; and NOTE: It is an implementation option to handle the receipt of the Floor Ack message and what action to take if the Floor Ack message is not received. c. shall remain in the 'G: Floor Taken' state; and if the active floor request queue is not empty: shall remove the queued floor requests of the users indicated in the List of Queued Userss field from the active floor request queue if the List of Queued Users field is present. Otherwise, shall remove all the queued floor requests from the active floor request queue; shall send a Queued Floor Requests message, including a Queued Floor Requests Purpose field with the Queued Floor Requests Purpose value set to '2' (Cancel Notification), to the associated floor participants whose floor requests have been removed from the queue and message is generated as described in the clause 8.2.15; shall send a Queued Floor Requests message to the associated floor participant as described in clause 8.2.15. The Queued Floor Requests: shall include a Queued Floor Requests Purpose field with the Queued Floor Requests Purpose value set to '1' (Cancel Result); shall include a Queued Floor Requests Result field with the Queued Floor Requests Result Value set to: '0' (Successfully removed the queued floor requests of all users specified in the List of Queued Users field in a message or all the queued floor requests from the floor request queue); '3' (The queued floor requests of all users specified in the List of Queued Users field in a message do not exist in the floor request queue); '4' (Unable to remove some of the queued floor requests of the users specified in the List of Queued Users field in a message); '5' (The queued floor requests of some of the users specified in the List of Queued Users field in a message do not exist in the floor request queue); or '255' (Unknown reason); if the Floor Request included a Track Info field, shall include the received Track Info field; and if the List of Queued Users field was present in the received message, shall include into the List of Queued Users field the list of users whose queued floor requests were not possible to remove and their queued requests still exist in the active floor request queue; and may send a Floor Queue Position Info message to the remaining users whose queued floor request position has changed in the active floor request queue, and if negotiated support of queueing of floor requests as specified in clause 14. The Floor Queue Position Info message: shall include the queue position and floor priority in the Queue Info field; and if the Floor Request message included a Track Info field, shall include the received Track Info field; and shall remain in the 'G: Floor Taken' state.
