Content: Upon receiving the Floor Idle message from the floor control server arbitration logic in the MCPTT server, the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server: if the G-bit in the Floor Indicator is set to '1' (Dual Floor) and an indication that the participant is overridden without revoke is stored shall send Floor Idle message to the associated floor participant; shall remove the indication that a participant is overridden without revoke; and shall remain in 'U: permitted state'; if no indication is stored shall enter the 'U: not permitted and Floor Idle' state as specified in the clause 6.3.5.3.2; and if an indication that the participant is overriding without revoke is stored shall send Floor Idle message to the associated floor participant; shall remove the indication that a participant is overriding without revoke; and shall remain in 'U: permitted state'.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State: 'U: permitted' > Send Floor Revoke message (S: Floor Revoke)
Content: When receiving the Floor Revoke message from the floor control server arbitration logic in the MCPTT server, the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server: shall forward the Floor Revoke message to the floor participant; if the Floor Revoke message includes the Track Info field, shall store the Track Info field; and shall enter the state 'U pending Floor Revoke' as specified in the clause 6.3.5.6.2.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State: 'U: permitted' > Receive RTP media packets (R: media)
Content: Upon receiving an indication from the network media interface in the MCPTT server that RTP media packets with payload are received from the associated floor participant, the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server: if an indication that the participant is overriding without revoke is not stored, shall request the network media interface in the MCPTT server to forward RTP media packets to the media distributor in the MCPTT server; and shall remain in the 'U: permitted' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State: 'U: permitted' > Receive Floor Request message (R: Floor Request)
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Request message from the associated floor participant, the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server: shall forward the Floor Request message to the floor control server arbitration logic in the MCPTT server; and shall instruct the media distributor to act as in clause 6.3.4.4.5. if an indication that the participant is overriding without revoke is stored, shall request the network media interface in the MCPTT server to forward RTP media packets to the media distributor in the MCPTT server; and shall instruct the media distributor to act as in clause 6.3.6.3.5; and shall remain in the 'U: permitted' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State: 'U: permitted' > Send RTP Media (S: media)
Content: When RTP packets are received from the media distributor, the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server: shall forward the RTP packet to the associated floor participant if the indication that the participant is overridden without revoke is stored; shall forward the RTP packet to the associated floor participant if the indication that the participant is overriding without revoke is stored; and shall remain in the 'U: permitted' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State: 'U: permitted' > Send Floor Taken message (S: Floor Taken)
Content: When receiving the Floor Taken message from the floor control server arbitration logic in the MCPTT server with the G-bit in the Floor Indicator set to '1' (Dual Floor), the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server: shall send the Floor Taken message to the associated floor participant; shall store an indication that the participant is overridden without revoke; and shall remain in the 'U: permitted' state. When receiving the Floor Taken message from the floor control server arbitration logic in the MCPTT server with the G-bit in the Floor Indicator set to '0' (Not dual floor), the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server: shall send the Floor Taken message to the associated floor participant; and shall enter the 'U: not permitted and Floor Taken' state as specified in the clause 6.3.5.4.2. When receiving the Floor Taken message from the floor control server arbitration logic in the MCPTT server with the I-bit in the Floor Indicator set to '1' (multi-talker), the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server: shall send the Floor Taken message to the associated floor participant; shall store an indication that the participant is listening to media from more than one source; and shall remain in the 'U: permitted' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State: 'U: permitted' > Send Floor Release Multi Talker message (S: Floor Release Multi Talker)
Content: When a Floor Release Multi Talker message is received from the floor control arbitration logic in the MCPTT server, the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server: shall forward the Floor Release Multi Talker message to the associated floor participant; and shall remain in the 'U: permitted' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State: 'U: permitted' > Receive Queued Floor Requests message (R: Queued Floor Requests)
Content: Upon receiving a Queued Floor Requests message, including a Queued Floor Requests Purpose field with the Queued Floor Requests Purpose value set to '0' (Cancel Request), from the associated floor participant: if the MCPTT ID of the associated floor participant is an authorized user (e.g dispatcher) to cancel the queued floor request of other MCPTT users (the list of specified users in a request or all the users), the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server: shall forward the Queued Floor Requests message to the floor control server arbitration logic; and shall remain in the 'U: permitted' state; and if the MCPTT ID of the associated floor participant is not an authorized user (If participant type is not dispatcher, dispatch supervisor or MC service administrator) to cancel the queued floor request of other MCPTT users (the list of specified users in a request or all the users), the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server: shall send a Queued Floor Requests message to the associated floor participant as described in clause 8.2.15. The Queued FloorRequests message: shall include a Queued Floor Requests Purpose field with the Queued Floor Requests Purpose value set to '1' (Cancel Result); shall include in the Queued Floor Requests Result Value set to '1' (Not authorized to remove the queued floor requests of all users specified in the List of Queued Users field in a message or all the queued floor requests from the floor request queue); and if the Floor Request included a Track Info field, shall include the received Track Info field; may set the first bit in the subtype of the Queued Floor Requests message to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2; and NOTE: It is an implementation option to handle the receipt of the Floor Ack message and what action to take if the Floor Ack message is not received. c. shall remain in the 'U: permitted' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State: 'U: permitted' > Send Queued Floor Requests message (S: Queued Floor Requests)
Content: Upon receiving a Queued Floor Requests message, including a Queued Floor Requests Purpose field with the Queued Floor Requests Purpose value set to '1' (Cancel Result), from the floor control server arbitration logic in the MCPTT server, the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server: shall forward the Queued Floor Requests message to the floor participant; and shall remain in the 'U: permitted' state. When a Queued Floor Requests message including a Queued Floor Requests Purpose field with the Queued Floor Requests Purpose value set to '2' (Cancel Notification), is received from the floor control arbitration logic in the MCPTT server, the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server: shall forward the Queued Floor Requests message to the associated floor participant; may set the first bit in the subtype of the Queued Floor Requests message to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2; and NOTE: It is an implementation option to handle the receipt of the Floor Ack message and what action to take if the Floor Ack message is not received. 3. shall remain in the 'U: permitted' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State: 'U: pending Floor Revoke' > General
Content: The floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server uses this state during the grace period after sending the Floor Revoke message. In this state timer T8 (Floor Revoke) is running.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State: 'U: pending Floor Revoke' > Enter state 'U pending Floor Revoke'
Content: When entering this state the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server: shall start timer T8 (Floor Revoke); and shall enter the state 'U: pending Floor Revoke'.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State: 'U: pending Floor Revoke' > Timer T8 (media Revoke) expired
Content: On expiry of timer T8 (Floor Revoke) the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server: shall retransmit the Floor Revoke message to the associated floor participant. The Floor Revoke message: shall include the same Rejection Cause field and the same Floor Indicator field as in the previous sent Floor Revoke message; shall start timer T8 (Floor Revoke); and shall remain in the 'U: pending Floor Revoke' state. NOTE: The number of times the floor control server retransmits the Floor Revoke message and the action to take when the floor control server gives up is an implementation option. However, it is recommended that the MCPTT client is disconnected from the MCPTT call when the floor control server gives up.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State: 'U: pending Floor Revoke' > Receive RTP media packets (R: media)
Content: Upon receiving an RTP media packet with payload from the associated floor participant, the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server: shall forward RTP media packets to the media distributor; and shall remain in the 'U: pending Floor Revoke' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State: 'U: pending Floor Revoke' > Receive Floor Release message (R: Floor Release)
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Release message from the associated floor participant, the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server: if the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Release message is set to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2, shall send a Floor Ack message. The Floor Ack message: shall include the Message Type field set to '4' (Floor Release); shall include the Source field set to '2' (the controlling MCPTT function is the source); and if the Floor Release message included a Track Info field, shall include the received Track Info field; if the G-bit in the Floor Indicator is set to '1' (Dual floor): if the state in the 'general floor control operation' state machine is 'G: Taken': shall send a Floor Taken message to the associated floor participant. The Floor Taken message: shall include the granted MCPTT user's MCPTT ID in the Granted Party's Identity field of the permitted MCPTT client and may include the functional alias of the granted MCPTT user in the Functional Alias field, if privacy is not requested; if a group call is a broadcast group call, a system call, an emergency call, an imminent peril call, or a temporary group session, shall include the Floor Indicator field with appropriate indications; and if the Floor Release message included a Track Info field, shall include the received Track Info field; and shall include the generated audio SSRC to be used by the granted MCPTT participant into the Audio SSRC of Granted Participant field; and shall enter the 'U: not permitted and Floor Taken' state as specified in the clause 6.3.5.4.2; and if the state in the 'general floor control operation' state machine is 'G: Idle': shall send a Floor Idle message to the associated floor participant; if a group call is a broadcast group call, a system call, an emergency call, an imminent peril call, or a temporary group session, shall include the Floor Indicator field with appropriate indications; if the Floor Release message included a Track Info field, shall include the received Track Info field; and shall enter the 'U: not permitted and Floor Idle' state as specified in the clause 6.3.5.3.2; and if the G-bit in the Floor Indicator is set to '0': shall forward the Floor Release message to the floor control server arbitration logic; and shall remain in the state 'U: pending Floor Revoke'.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State: 'U: pending Floor Revoke' > Send Floor Idle message (S: Floor Idle)
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Idle message from the floor control server arbitration logic in the MCPTT server, the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server: NOTE 1: The Floor Idle message is sent when timer T3 (Stop talking grace) expires and when timer T1 (End of RTP media) expires and when there are no queued floor requests. shall send the Floor Idle message to the associated floor participant; may set the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Idle message to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2; and NOTE 2: It is an implementation option to handle the receipt of the Floor Ack message and what action to take if the Floor Ack message is not received. 3. shall enter the 'U: not permitted and Floor Idle' state as specified in the clause 6.3.5.3.2.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State: 'U: pending Floor Revoke' > Send Floor Taken message (S: Floor Taken)
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Taken message from the floor control server arbitration logic in the MCPTT server, the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server: NOTE 1: The Floor Taken message is sent when timer T3 (Stop talking grace) expires or when timer T1 (End of RTP media) expires and if there are queued floor requests. shall send the Floor Taken message to the associated floor participant; may set the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Taken message to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2; and NOTE 2: It is an implementation option to handle the receipt of the Floor Ack message and what action to take if the Floor Ack message is not received. 3. shall enter the 'U: not permitted and Floor Taken' state as specified in the clause 6.3.5.4.2.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State: 'U: pending Floor Revoke' > Send Floor Release Multi Talker message (S: Floor Release Multi Talker)
Content: When a Floor Release Multi Talker message is received from the floor control arbitration logic in the MCPTT server, the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server: shall forward the Floor Release Multi Talker message to the associated floor participant; and shall remain in the 'U: pending Floor Revoke' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State 'U: not permitted but sends media' > General
Content: The floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server uses this state when it receives RTP media packets from the MCPTT client and the MCPTT client is not permitted to send media. Timer T8 (Floor Revoke) is running in this state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State 'U: not permitted but sends media' > Enter state 'U: not permitted but sends media'
Content: When entering this state the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server: shall start timer T8 (Floor Revoke); and shall enter the state 'U: not permitted but sends media'. In this state the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server: 1. shall not request the network media interface in the MCPTT server to forward RTP media packets from the MCPTT client to the media distributor in the MCPTT server.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State 'U: not permitted but sends media' > Timer T8 (Floor Revoke) expired
Content: On expiry of timer T8 (Floor Revoke), the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server: shall send a Floor Revoke message to the associated floor participant. The Floor Revoke message: shall include in the Rejection Cause field the Rejection Cause value set to \#3 (No permission to send a Media Burst); if a group call is a broadcast group call, a system call, an emergency call, an imminent peril call, or a temporary group session, shall include the Floor Indicator field with appropriate indications; and if a Track Info field was included in the Floor Request message, shall include the stored Track Info field; and shall restart timer T8 (Floor Revoke); and shall remain in the 'U: not permitted but sends media' state. NOTE: The number of times the floor control server retransmits the Floor Revoke message and the action to take when the floor control server gives up is an implementation option. However, the recommended action is that the MCPTT client is disconnected from the MCPTT call.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State 'U: not permitted but sends media' > Receive Floor Release message (R: Floor Release)
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Release message, the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server: if the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Release message is set to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2, shall send a Floor Ack message. The Floor Ack message: shall include the Message Type field set to '4' (Floor Release); shall include the Source field set to '2' (the controlling MCPTT function is the source); and if the Floor Release message included a Track Info field, shall include the received Track Info field; if the general state is 'G: Floor Idle', the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server: shall send the Floor Idle message. The Floor Idle message: shall include a Message Sequence Number field with a Message Sequence Number value increased with 1; if a group call is a broadcast group call, a system call, an emergency call, an imminent peril call, or a temporary group session, shall include the Floor Indicator field with appropriate indications; and if the Floor Release message included a Track Info field, shall include the received Track Info field; and shall enter the 'U: not permitted and Floor Idle' state as specified in the clause 6.3.5.3.2; and if the general state is 'G: Floor Taken', the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server: shall send a Floor Taken message. The Floor Taken message: shall include the granted MCPTT user's MCPTT ID in the Granted Party's Identity field and may include the functional alias of the granted MCPTT user in the Functional Alias field, if privacy is not requested; if the session is a broadcast group call, shall include the Permission to Request the floor field set to '0'; if the session is not a broadcast group call, may include the Permission to Request the floor field set to '1'; may include the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Taken message set to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2; NOTE: It is an implementation option to handle the receipt of the Floor Ack message and what action to take if the Floor Ack message is not received. [] if a group call is a broadcast group call, a system call, an emergency call, an imminent peril call, or a temporary group session, shall include the Floor Indicator field with appropriate indications; if the Floor Release message included a Track Info field, shall include the received Track Info field; and shall include the generated audio SSRC to be used by the granted MCPTT participant into the Audio SSRC of Granted Participant field; and shall enter the 'U: not permitted and Floor Taken' state as specified in the clause 6.3.5.4.2.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State 'U: not permitted but sends media' > Send Floor Taken message (S: Floor Taken)
Content: When receiving the Floor Taken message from the floor control server arbitration logic in the MCPTT server with the I-bit in the Floor Indicator set to '1' (multi-talker), the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server: shall send the Floor Taken message to the associated floor participant; shall store an indication that the participant is listening to media from more than one source; and shall remain in the 'U: not permitted but sends media' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State 'U: not permitted but sends media' > Send Floor Release Multi Talker message (S: Floor Release Multi Talker)
Content: When a Floor Release Multi Talker message is received from the floor control arbitration logic in the MCPTT server, the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server: shall forward the Floor Release Multi Talker message to the associated floor participant; and shall remain in the 'U: not permitted but sends media' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > In any state > General
Content: This clause describes the actions to be taken in all states defined for the basic state diagram with the exception of the 'Start-stop' and 'Releasing' states.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > In any state > Receive MCPTT call release 1
Content: Upon receiving an MCPTT call release step 1 request from the application and signalling plane e.g. when the MCPTT call is going to be released or when the MCPTT client leaves the MCPTT call, the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server: shall stop sending floor control messages to the associated floor participant; shall request the network media interface to stop sending RTP media packets towards to the associated MCPTT client; shall ignore any floor control messages received from the associated floor participant; shall request the network media interface to stop forwarding RTP media packets from the associated MCPTT client to the media distributor in the MCPTT server; shall indicate to the floor control server arbitration logic in the MCPTT server that the MCPTT client has started to disconnect from the MCPTT call; and shall enter the 'Releasing' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > In any state > Receiving a merging instruction (R: Merge)
Content: Upon receipt of an instruction to merge with another group due to the group regrouping function, the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client: shall create an instance of the 'floor participant interface state transition' as specified in clause 6.5.5; shall move information associated with the instance used for 'basic floor control operation towards the floor participant' to the 'floor participant interface state transition' state machine; NOTE: Which information that needs to be moved is an implementation option. shall enter the 'Start-stop' state and terminate the 'basic floor control operation towards the floor participant'' state machine associated with this floor participant and this MCPTT call; if the state was 'U: not permitted and Floor Idle', 'U: not permitted Floor Taken', 'U: pending Floor Revoke', 'U: not permitted and initiating' or 'U: not permitted but sends media': shall enter the 'P: has no permission' state as specified in clause 6.5.5; and shall perform actions specified in clause 6.5.5.3; and if the state was 'U: permitted': shall enter the 'P: has permission' state; and shall perform actions specified in clause 6.5.5.4.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State: 'Releasing' > General
Content: The floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server uses this state while waiting for the application and signalling plane to finalize the release of the MCPTT call or finalizing the removal of the MCPTT client from the MCPTT call.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State: 'Releasing' > Receive MCPTT call release - 2
Content: Upon receiving an MCPTT call release step 2 request from the application and signalling plane, the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server: shall request the network media interface to release all resources associated with this MCPTT client for this MCPTT call; and shall enter the 'Start-stop' state and terminate the 'basic floor control operation towards the floor participant'' state machine associated with this floor participant and this MCPTT call.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State: 'U: not permitted and initiating' > General
Content: The floor control interface towards the MCPTT client uses this state when waiting for the floor control arbitration logic to finalize the initialisation of the state machine to be used for a temporary group session. During this state Floor Request messages can be received from the non-controlling MCPTT function. Any Floor Request message received will be added to the queue according to the priority of the floor request determine as described in clause 4.1.1.4.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State: 'U: not permitted and initiating' > Enter the 'U: not permitted and initiating' state
Content: The floor control interface towards the MCPTT client: 1. shall set the state for the associated floor participant to 'U: not permitted and Initiating'.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State: 'U: not permitted and initiating' > Send Floor Taken message (S: Floor Taken)
Content: When a Floor Taken message is received from the floor control arbitration logic in the MCPTT server, the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client: shall forward the Floor Taken messages to the associated floor participant; may set the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Taken message to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2; and NOTE: It is an implementation option to handle the receipt of the Floor Ack message and what action to take if the Floor Ack message is not received. 3. shall enter the state 'U: not permitted and Floor Taken' as specified in clause 6.3.5.4.2.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State: 'U: not permitted and initiating' > Send Floor Idle message (S: Floor Idle)
Content: When receiving a Floor Idle message from the floor control server arbitration logic in the MCPTT server, the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client: shall forward the Floor Idle message to the associated floor participant; may set the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Idle message to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2; and NOTE: It is an implementation option to handle the receipt of the Floor Ack message and what action to take if the Floor Ack message is not received. 3. shall enter the 'U: not permitted and Floor Idle' state as specified in the clause 6.3.5.3.2.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State: 'U: not permitted and initiating' > Receive Floor Request message (R: Floor Request)
Content: Upon receipt of a Floor Request message, the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client: shall determine the effective priority level as described in clause 4.1.1.4; shall put the Floor Request message in the active floor request queue according to the determined effective priority level; if the Queueing Capability value in the Track Info field is set to '1' (the floor participant in the MCPTT client supports queueing), shall send a Floor Queue Position Info message to the requesting non-Controlling MCPTT function, The Floor Queue Position Info message: shall include the queue position and floor priority in the Queue Info field; shall include the received Track Info field; and if a group call is a broadcast group call, a system call, an emergency call, an imminent peril call, or a temporary group session, shall include the Floor Indicator field with appropriate indications; if the Queueing Capability value in the Track Info field is set to '0' (the floor participant in the MCPTT client does not support queueing), shall send the Floor Deny message. The floor Deny message: [NOTE:]A Floor Request from a MCPTT client in a constituent group can be received without the queueuing capability if a floor participant in an ongoing constituent MCPTT group request floor while the floor was idle during the merging process. shall include in the Reject Cause field the Reject Cause value cause '1' (Another MCPTT client has permission); shall include the received Track Info field; and if a group call is a broadcast group call, a system call, an emergency call, an imminent peril call, or a temporary group session, shall include the Floor Indicator field with appropriate indications; and shall remain in the 'U: not permitted and initiating' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State: 'U: not permitted and initiating' > Send Floor Granted message (S: Floor Granted)
Content: When a Floor Granted message is received from the floor control arbitration logic, the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client: shall forward the Floor Granted messages to the associated floor participant; may set the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Granted message to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2; and NOTE: It is an implementation option to handle the receipt of the Floor Ack message and what action to take if the Floor Ack message is not received. 3. shall enter the state 'U: permitted' as specified in clause 6.3.5.5.2.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State: 'U: not permitted and initiating' > Receive a Floor Release message (S: Floor Release)
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Release message from the associated floor participant, the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client: if the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Release message is set to '1' (Acknowledgment is required) as described in clause 8.2.2, shall send a Floor Ack message. The Floor Ack message: shall include the Message Type field set to '4' (Floor Release); shall include the Source field set to '2' (the controlling MCPTT function is the source); and if the Floor Release message included a Track Info field, shall include the received Track Info field; shall use the topmost Participant Reference value and the SSRC in the Track Info field of the received Floor Release message to check if the floor participant has a queued floor request and if not, check if there is a floor request in one of the cached application/vnd.3gpp.mcptt-floor-request+xml MIME bodies; shall remove the MCPTT client from the active floor request queue or the cached application/vnd.3gpp.mcptt-floor-request+xml MIME body, if the MCPTT client was in the active floor request queue or in the application/vnd.3gpp.mcptt-floor-request+xml MIME body; and shall remain in the 'U: not permitted and initiating' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Floor control server state transition diagram for basic floor control operation towards the floor participant > State: 'U: not permitted and initiating' > Send Floor Release Multi Talker message (S: Floor Release Multi Talker)
Content: When a Floor Release Multi Talker message is received from the floor control arbitration logic in the MCPTT server, the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client in the floor control server: shall forward the Floor Release Multi Talker message to the associated floor participant; and shall remain in the ''U: not permitted and initiating' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Dual floor control > General
Content: Dual floor control is used when a floor participant requests floor with a pre-emptive floor priority allowing the floor control in clause 6.3.4 to continue without revoking the floor from the MCPTT client permitted to send media as specified in 3GPP TS 23.379 clause 10.9.1.3.2.2. The floor control server arbitration logic in the floor control server shall behave according to the state diagram and state transitions specified in this clause. Figure 6.3.6.1-1 shows the 'dual floor control operation' states (D states) and the state transition diagram. The floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server shall keep one instance of the 'dual floor control operation' state machine for a floor participant with pre-emptive floor priority where this MCPTT user is allowed to talk without revoking a current speaker with lower flow priority. NOTE: Only one MCPTT user with this type of pre-emptive floor priority can exist within an MCPTT call. The floor participant associated to the 'dual floor control operation' state machine is in the following clauses referred to as the overriding MCPTT client. If floor control messages or RTP media packets arrives in a state where there is no procedure specified in the following clauses the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: shall discard the floor control message; shall request the media distributor in the MCPTT server to discard any received RTP media packet; and shall remain in the current state. State details are explained in the following clauses.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Dual floor control > State: 'Start-stop' > General
Content: When a new instance of the 'dual floor control operation' state machine is initiated, before any floor control related input is applied, the state machine is in 'Start-stop' state. Similarly when the media burst is released the state machine shall return to the 'Start-Stop' state or the related MCPTT call is released.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Dual floor control > State: 'Start-stop' > Receive Floor Request message with overriding pre-emptive floor priority (R: Floor Request)
Content: When a floor participant request floor with an overriding pre-emptive priority is granted the floor the floor control arbitration logic: shall create one instance of the 'dual floor control operation' state machine; shall store the SSRC of floor participant; if a Track Info field is included in the Floor Request message, shall store the received Track Info field associated with the floor control server state transition diagram for 'dual floor control operation', and shall enter the state 'D: Floor Taken' as specified in the clause 6.3.6.3.2.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Dual floor control > State: 'D: Floor Taken' > General
Content: The floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server uses this state when it has permitted one floor participant to override a current speaker in an MCPTT call to send media. Timer T11 (End of RTP dual) can be running when the floor control server is in this state. Timer T12 (Stop talking dual) can be running when the floor control server is in this state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Dual floor control > State: 'D: Floor Taken' > Enter state 'D: Floor Taken'
Content: When entering this state the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: shall send a Floor Granted message to the requesting floor participant. The Floor Granted message: shall include the value of the T12 (Stop talking dual) timer in the Duration field; shall include the granted priority in the Floor priority field; if a Track Info field associated with the floor control server state transition diagram for 'dual floor control operation' is stored, shall include the stored Track Info field; shall include the Floor Indicator field with the G-bit set to '1' (Dual floor); if a group call is a broadcast group call, a system call, an emergency call, an imminent peril call, or a temporary group session, shall include the Floor Indicator field with appropriate indications; and f . shall include in the Audio SSRC of Granted Participant field a globally unique audio SSRC, generated and stored by the floor control server, to be used by the granted MCPTT participant in the RTP media packets it will send; shall send a Floor Idle message to any non-controlling MCPTT functions involved in the session and to those floor participants controlled by the controlling MCPTT function that will only listen to RTP media from the overriding MCPTT client according to local policy. The Floor Idle message: [NOTE 1:]The non-controlling MCPTT function will send the Floor Idle message to those floor participants controlled by the non-controlling MCPTT function that will only listen to RTP media from the overriding MCPTT client according to local policy when the non-controlling MCPTT function receives the Floor Taken with the G-bit set to '1' (Dual floor) in the Floor Indicator field. shall include a Message Sequence Number field with a Message Sequence Number value increased with 1; and if a group call is a broadcast group call, a system call, an emergency call, an imminent peril call, or a temporary group session, shall include the Floor Indicator field with appropriate indications; shall send a Floor Taken message to any non-controlling MCPTT functions involved and to floor participants controlled by the controlling MCPTT function that will listen to the RTP media from the overriding MCPTT client according to local policy. The Floor Taken message: NOTE 2: The MCPTT client overridden by the overriding MCPTT client is still sending voice (overridden). The list of floor participants that receive the overriding, overridden, or both transmissions is based on configuration. shall include the granted MCPTT user's MCPTT ID in the Granted Party's Identity field and may include the functional alias of the granted MCPTT user in the Functional Alias field, if privacy is not requested; shall include a Message Sequence Number field with a Message Sequence Number value increased with 1; shall include the Floor Indicator field with the G-bit set to '1' (Dual floor); if a group call is a broadcast group call, a system call, an emergency call, an imminent peril call, or a temporary group session, shall include the Floor Indicator field with appropriate indications; and shall include the generated audio SSRC to be used by the granted MCPTT participant into the Audio SSRC of Granted Participant field; shall start the T11 (End of RTP dual) timer; and shall enter the state to 'D: Floor Taken' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Dual floor control > State: 'D: Floor Taken' > Timer T11 (End of RTP dual) expired
Content: On expiry of timer T11 (End of RTP dual), the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: shall stop the T12 (Stop talking dual) timer; if running; shall request the media distributor in the MCPTT server to stop distributing RTP media packets received from the overriding MCPTT client to other MCPTT clients; NOTE: If dual floor control is ongoing as described in clause 6.3.6, the list of floor participants that receive the overriding, overridden, or both transmissions is based on configuration. shall release all resources reserved in the media plane including the instances used for the 'dual floor control operation' state machine and any running timers associated with the state machine; shall send a Floor Idle message to any non-controlling MCPTT functions and to those floor participants controlled by the controlling MCPTT functions receiving RTP media from the overriding MCPTT client. The Floor Idle message: shall include a Floor Indicator field with the G-bit set to '1' (Dual floor); shall include a Message Sequence Number field with a Message Sequence Number value increased with 1; and if a group call is a broadcast group call, a system call, an emergency call, an imminent peril call, or a temporary group session, shall include the Floor Indicator field with appropriate indications; if the state in the 'general floor control operation' state machine is 'G: Taken'; shall send a Floor Taken message to any non-controlling MCPTT functions and to those floor participants controlled by the controlling MCPTT function that only received RTP media from the overriding MCPTT client. The Floor Taken message: shall include the granted MCPTT user's MCPTT ID in the Granted Party's Identity field of the permitted MCPTT client and may include the functional alias of the granted MCPTT user in the Functional Alias field, if privacy is not requested; shall include a Message Sequence Number field with a Message Sequence Number value increased with 1; if a group call is a broadcast group call, a system call, an emergency call, an imminent peril call, or a temporary group session, shall include the Floor Indicator field with appropriate indications; and shall include the generated audio SSRC to be used by the granted MCPTT participant into the Audio SSRC of Granted Participant field; if the state in the 'general floor control operation' state machine is 'G: Idle', shall send a Floor Idle message to all floor participants. The Floor Idle message: shall include a Message Sequence Number field with a Message Sequence Number value increased with 1; and if a group call is a broadcast group call, a system call, an emergency call, an imminent peril call, or a temporary group session, shall include the Floor Indicator field with appropriate indications; and shall enter the 'Start-stop' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Dual floor control > State: 'D: Floor Taken' > Timer T12 (Stop talking dual) expired
Content: On expiry of the T12 (Stop talking dual) timer the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: shall stop the T11 (End of RTP dual) timer; shall request the media distributor in the MCPTT server to stop distributing RTP media packets to other MCPTT client; shall send the Floor Revoke message to the permitted participant. The Floor Revoke message: shall include the Reject Cause field with the Reject Cause value set to \#2 (Media burst too long) in the Floor Revoke message sent in clause 6.3.4.5.2; shall include the Floor Indicator with the G-bit set to '1' (Dual floor); if a Track Info field associated with the dual floor is stored, shall include the stored Track Info field; and if a group call is a broadcast group call, a system call, an emergency call, an imminent peril call, or a temporary group session, shall include the Floor Indicator field with appropriate indications; shall remove the stored Track info field associated with the dual floor; if the state in the 'general floor control operation' state machine is 'G: Taken'; shall send a Floor Taken message to all non-controlling functions (if the session is a temporary group session involving non-controlling MCPTT functions) and to those floor participants that only received RTP media from the overriding MCPTT client. The Floor Taken message: shall include the granted MCPTT user's MCPTT ID in the Granted Party's Identity field of the permitted MCPTT client and may include the functional alias of the granted MCPTT user in the Functional Alias field, if privacy is not requested; shall include a Message Sequence Number field with a Message Sequence Number value increased with 1; and shall include the generated audio SSRC to be used by the granted MCPTT participant into the Audio SSRC of Granted Participant field; if the state in the 'general floor control operation' state machine is 'G: Idle', shall send a Floor Idle message to all floor participants. The Floor Idle message: shall include a Message Sequence Number field with a Message Sequence Number value increased with 1; and if a group call is a broadcast group call, a system call, an emergency call, an imminent peril call, or a temporary group session, shall include the Floor Indicator field with appropriate indications; shall release all resources reserved in the media plane including the instances used for the Floor control server state transition diagram for 'dual floor control operation'; and shall enter the 'Start-stop' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Dual floor control > State: 'D: Floor Taken' > Receive RTP media packets (R: media)
Content: Upon receiving an indication from the media distributor in the MCPTT server that RTP media packets are received from the permitted MCPTT client, the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: shall start the T12 (Stop talking dual) timer, if not already running; shall restart the T11 (End of RTP dual) timer; shall instruct the media distributor to forward the received RTP media packets to any non-controlling MCPTT functions, to the overridden MCPTT client and to those MCPTT clients receiving RTP media from the overriding MCPTT client controlled by the controlling MCPTT functions according to local policy; and [NOTE 1:]The RTP media is sent to the MCPTT clients subjected to the maximum number of simultaneous transmissions received in one group call for override (N7) as indicated in 3GPP TS 23.379 . [NOTE 2:]The non-controlling MCPTT function will forward the RTP media packets to MCPTT clients receiving RTP media from the overriding MCPTT client according to local policy in the non-controlling MCPTT function. 4. shall remain in the 'D: Floor Taken' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Dual floor control > State: 'D: Floor Taken' > Receive Floor Release message (R: Floor Release)
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Release message the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: shall request the media distributor in the MCPTT server to stop distributing RTP media packets received from the overriding MCPTT client to other MCPTT client; shall stop the T12 (Stop talking dual) timer, if running; shall stop the T11 (End of RTP dual) timer; shall release all resources reserved in the media plane including the instances used for the Floor control server state transition diagram for 'dual floor control operation' and any running timers associated with the state machine; if the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Release message is set to '1' (acknowledgement is required) as specified in clause 8.2.2: shall send a Floor Ack message. The Floor Ack message: shall set the Source field to the value '2' (the controlling MCPTT function is the source); and shall set the Message Type field to the value '4'( Floor Release); shall send a Floor Idle message to any non-controlling MCPTT functions, to the overridden floor participant and to those floor participants controlled by the controlling MCPTT functions receiving RTP media from the overriding MCPTT client. The Floor Idle message: shall include an Floor Indicator field with the G-bit set to '1' (Dual floor); shall include a Message Sequence Number field with a Message Sequence Number value increased with 1; and if a group call is a broadcast group call, a system call, an emergency call, an imminent peril call, or a temporary group session, shall include the Floor Indicator field with appropriate indications; if the state in the 'general floor control operation' state machine is 'G: Taken', shall send a Floor Taken message to any non-controlling MCPTT functions and to those floor participants that only received RTP media from the overriding MCPTT client. The Floor Taken message: shall include the granted MCPTT user's MCPTT ID in the Granted Party's Identity field of the permitted MCPTT client and may include the functional alias of the granted MCPTT user in the Functional Alias field, if privacy is not requested; shall include a Message Sequence Number field with a Message Sequence Number value increased with 1; if a group call is a broadcast group call, a system call, an emergency call, an imminent peril call, or a temporary group session, shall include the Floor Indicator field with appropriate indications; and shall include the generated audio SSRC to be used by the granted MCPTT participant into the Audio SSRC of Granted Participant field; if the state in the 'general floor control operation' state machine is 'G: Idle', shall send a Floor Idle message to all floor participants;. The Floor Idle message: shall include a Message Sequence Number field with a Message Sequence Number value increased with 1; and if a group call is a broadcast group call, a system call, an emergency call, an imminent peril call, or a temporary group session, shall include the Floor Indicator field with appropriate indications; and shall enter the 'Start-stop' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Dual floor control > State: 'D: Floor Taken' > Receive Floor request message from permitted floor participant (R: Floor Request)
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Request message from the floor participant that has been granted permission to send overriding media, the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: shall send a Floor Granted message to the previously granted floor participant. The Floor Granted message: shall include the value of the T12 (Stop talking dual) timer in the Duration field; shall include the granted priority in the Floor priority field; if the Floor Request message included a Track Info field, shall include the received Track Info field; and if a group call is a broadcast group call, a system call, an emergency call, an imminent peril call, or a temporary group session, shall include the Floor Indicator field with appropriate indications; and shall remain in the 'D: Floor Taken' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Dual floor control > State: 'D: Floor Taken' > Permitted MCPTT client release
Content: If the floor control server receives an indication from the floor control interface towards the MCPTT client that the MCPTT client has started to disconnect from the MCPTT call, the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: shall request the media distributor in the MCPTT server to stop distributing RTP media packets received from the overriding MCPTT client to other MCPTT clients; shall send a Floor Idle message to any non-controlling MCPTT functions and to those floor participants controlled by the controlling MCPTT functions receiving RTP media from the overriding MCPTT client. The Floor Idle message: shall include an Floor Indicator field with the G-bit set to '1' (Dual floor); shall include a Message Sequence Number field with a Message Sequence Number value increased with 1; and if a group call is a broadcast group call, a system call, an emergency call, an imminent peril call, or a temporary group session, shall include the Floor Indicator field with appropriate indications; shall release all resources reserved in the media plane including the instances used for the Floor control server state transition diagram for 'dual floor control operation' and any running timers associated with the state machine; and shall enter the 'Start-stop' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Dual floor control > State: 'D: Floor Taken' > Receive Terminate (Terminate)
Content: Upon receiving the termination instruction, the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: shall set the value of timer T1 (End of RTP media) to the value of timer T11 (End of RTP dual) and start timer T1, if timer T11 (End of RTP dual) is running; shall set the value of timer T2 (Stop talking) to the value of timer T12 (Stop talking dual) and start timer T2, if timer T12 (Stop talking dual) is running; shall release all resources reserved in the media plane including the instances used for the dual floor control operation' state machine and stop any running timers associated with the state machine; and shall enter the 'Start-stop' state. NOTE: The overriding participant continues to have the floor until its current talk burst is terminated.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Dual floor control > In any state > General
Content: This clause describes the actions to be taken in all states defined for the general state diagram with the exception of the 'Start-stop' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Dual floor control > In any state > Receive MCPTT call release - 1
Content: This clause is used by the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server when an MCPTT call is released. Upon receiving an MCPTT call release step 1 request from the application and signalling plane the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: shall request the media distributor in the MCPTT server to stop sending RTP media packets MCPTT clients; and shall enter the 'Releasing' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Dual floor control > State: 'Releasing' > General
Content: The floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server uses this state while waiting for the application and signalling plane to finalize the disconnection of an MCPTT call.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Floor control server procedures > Dual floor control > State: 'Releasing' > Receive MCPTT call release - 2
Content: Upon receiving an MCPTT call release step 2 request from the application and signalling plane, the floor control arbitration logic in the floor control server: shall release all resources reserved in the media plane including the instances used for the dual floor control operation' state machine and any running timers associated with the state machine; and shall enter the 'Start-stop' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Participating MCPTT function floor control procedures > General
Content: Once an on-demand MCPTT session is established or a pre-established session is in use when the participating MCPTT function receives floor control messages from the floor participant in the MCPTT client or from the floor control server in the controlling MCPTT function, the behaviour of the participating MCPTT function is described in the following clauses.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Participating MCPTT function floor control procedures > Receive floor control messages
Content: Upon receiving a floor control message the participating MCPTT function: shall immediately forward the floor control message to the floor control server if the message is received from the floor participant; when forwarding the floor control message, the participating MCPTT function shall update the RTCP header of the floor control message with the RTCP SSRC it has received from the floor control server at session establishment. if an MBMS subchannel is not used for a conversation in the session the floor control message is associated with, shall immediately forward the floor control message to the floor participant if the message is received from the floor control server; and when forwarding the floor control message, the participating MCPTT function shall update the RTCP header of the floor control message with the RTCP SSRC it has received from the floor participant at session establishment. if an MBMS subchannel is used for a conversation in the session the floor control message is associated with: if the floor control message is not a Floor Idle message or a Floor Taken message or a Floor Release Multi Talker message; the MCPTT client has not reported "listening" status as specified in 3GPP TS 24.379 clause 14.2.3; the MCPTT client has reported "not-listening" status as specified in 3GPP TS 24.379 clause 14.2.3 in the latest received MBMS bearer listening status report; or the floor control message is a Floor Idle message or a Floor Taken message or a Floor Release Multi Talker message, protection of floor control messages sent over the MBMS subchannel from the participating MCPTT function to the served MCPTT clients is required, and the participating MCPTT function determined that the MCPTT client does not support MuSiK as specified in 3GPP TS 24.379 ; shall immediately forward the floor control message to the floor participant; and if the MCPTT client has reported "listening" status as specified in 3GPP TS 24.379 clause 14.2.3 in the latest received MBMS bearer listening status report; and if the floor control message is the Floor Idle message or the Floor Taken message or the Floor Release Multi Talker message, and: protection of floor control messages sent over the MBMS subchannel from the participating MCPTT function to the served MCPTT clients is not required ; or the participating MCPTT function determined that the MCPTT client supports MuSiK as specified in 3GPP TS 24.379 ; shall perform actions as specified in clause 10.2. NOTE: When the Floor Idle, Floor Taken, or Floor Release Multi Talker messages are discarded the messages are sent to the MCPTT clients over the MBMS subchannel allocated for the conversation as specified in clause 10.2.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Participating MCPTT function floor control procedures > Receive RTP media packets (R: RTP Media)
Content: Upon receiving RTP media packets the participating MCPTT function: shall immediately forward the RTP media packet to the controlling MCPTT function if the RTP packet is from an MCPTT client; and if an MBMS subchannel is not used for a conversation in the session the RTP media packets are associated with, shall immediately forward the RTP media packets to the MCPTT client if the RTP packet is from the controlling MCPTT function or the non-controlling MCPTT function. if an MBMS subchannel is used for a conversation in the session the RTP media packets are associated with and if RTP media packets are received from the controlling MCPTT function or the non-controlling MCPTT function: if the MCPTT client has not reported "listening" status as specified in 3GPP TS 24.379 clause 14.2.3; or the MCPTT client has reported "not-listening" status as specified in 3GPP TS 24.379 clause 14.2.3 in the latest received MBMS bearer listening status report, shall immediately forward the RTP media packets to the MCPTT client; and if the MCPTT client has reported "listening" status as specified in 3GPP TS 24.379 clause 14.2.3 in the latest received MBMS bearer listening status report, shall perform actions as specified in clause 10.2.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Participating MCPTT function floor control procedures > Release of session
Content: When the participating function receives an indication from the application and signalling plane that session release is initiated, the participating MCPTT function: shall stop sending floor control messages towards the floor participant and the floor control server; and shall stop sending RTP media packets towards the MCPTT client and towards the controlling MCPTT function. When the participating MCPTT function receives an indication from the application and signalling plane that the session is released, the participating MCPTT function: in case of a pre-established session, shall perform the actions in clause 9.3.2; and in case of an on-demand session, shall release the media resources associated with the session.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > General
Content: The floor control server interface in the non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group shall support the procedures in clauses 6.5.2, 6.5.3 and 6.5.4. The floor participant interface in the non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group shall support the procedures in clause 6.5.5.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > The MCPTT call initialization procedure in the non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > General
Content: The clause 6.5.2.2 describes the initial procedures when a new SIP session is establishing a group session. The clause 6.5.2.3 describes the procedure for switching from a controlling MCPTT function mode to a non-controlling MCPTT function mode.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > The MCPTT call initialization procedure in the non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Initial procedures when a new SIP session is establishing a group session
Content: When receiving an indication from the application and signalling plane that a group session is initiated, the floor control server interface: shall initiate and store a message sequence number value with the value to be used in the Message Sequence Number field in the Floor Idle and Floor Taken messages; shall for each MCPTT client in the MCPTT group controlled by the non-controlling MCPTT function that are participating in the session: generate a random temporary identifier between '0' and '4294967295'; store an association between the generated temporary identifier and the floor participant interface; store information about capabilities negotiated in the "mc_queueing" and "mc_priority" fmtp attributes as described in clause 14; store information whether the MCPTT client requested privacy or not; and initiate an instance of the 'floor participant interface state transition' state machine as specified in clause 6.5.5; and shall perform the actions in the clause 6.5.4. When receiving an indication from the application and signalling plane that an MCPTT client has accepted an invitation to the session, the floor participant interface shall perform the actions in clause 6.5.5.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > The MCPTT call initialization procedure in the non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Switching from a controlling MCPTT function mode to a non-controlling MCPTT function mode > Overview
Content: The switching from working in a controlling MCPTT functional mode to a non-controlling MCPTT functional mode is a 2-step procedure. [Step 1]The controlling MCPTT function prepares for acting as a non-controlling MCPTT function. The step 1 procedure is specified in clause 6.5.2.3.2. Before continuing with step 2 the application and signalling plane needs to receive a confirmation that the SIP session between the floor control server and the interface to the floor control server is established. [Step 2]The controlling MCPTT functions starts acting as a non-controlling MCPTT function. The step 2 procedure is specified in clause 6.5.2.3.3.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > The MCPTT call initialization procedure in the non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Switching from a controlling MCPTT function mode to a non-controlling MCPTT function mode > Preparing for the switch to non-controlling MCPTT function (Step 1)
Content: When receiving a request from the application and signalling plane to prepare for merging with another group session, the floor control server: 1. if in the 'G: taken' state, shall provide information about current speaker to the signalling and application plane; NOTE: The signalling and application plane will use the information about the current speaker to send a floor request in an SIP MESSAGE request as specified in 3GPP TS 24.379 . shall release the instant used for 'general floor control operation'; and shall for each MCPTT client in the MCPTT group controlled by the controlling MCPTT function and participating in the session: generate a random temporary identifier between '0' and '4294967295'; store an association between the generated temporary identifier and the floor participant interface; store information about capabilities negotiated in the "mc_queueing" and "mc_priority" fmtp attributes as specified in clause 14; store information whether the MCPTT client requested privacy or not; and initiate an instance of the 'floor participant interface state transition' state machine as specified in clause 6.3.5.8.3.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > The MCPTT call initialization procedure in the non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Switching from a controlling MCPTT function mode to a non-controlling MCPTT function mode > Start acting as a non-controlling MCPTT function (Step 2)
Content: When receiving a request from the application and signalling plane to finalize the switch to non-controlling MCPTT function behaviour, the floor control server: shall start acting as a floor control server interface; if an active floor request queue exists, for each queued floor request in the active floor request queue: NOTE: The active floor request queue was built up when the non-controlling MCPTT function was acting as a floor control server. shall send a Floor Request message to the floor control server. The Floor Request: shall include all fields included by the floor participant; if a Track Info field is included, shall include the temporary identifier at the end of the Floor Participant Reference value item; and if a Track Info field is not included, shall include a Track Info field populated as follows: shall include the "mc_queueing" fmtp attribute value negotiated as specified in clause 14 in the Queueing Capability value; shall include a Participant Type value based on the participant-type element specified in 3GPP TS 24.481 , if value in the participant-type element is available, otherwise set the Participant Type value to "unknown"; and C. shall include the temporary identifier as the first Floor Participant Reference value; if an active floor request queue exists, shall move the active floor request queue to a passive floor request queue; and shall perform the actions in the clause 6.5.4. When receiving an indication from the application and signalling plane that an MCPTT client has joined the session, the floor participant interface shall perform the actions in clause 6.5.5.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > The MCPTT call release procedure in the non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group
Content: When an MCPTT client leaves an MCPTT call and the MCPTT call remains ongoing with the other MCPTT clients, the non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group follows a two-step procedure: [Step 1]The floor participant interface stops sending floor control messages and RTP media packets to the MCPTT client leaving the MCPTT call and the floor participant interface discards floor control messages and RTP media packets received from the MCPTT client leaving the MCPTT call; and [Step 2]When the application and signalling plane has determined that the session with this floor participant has been released, the corresponding instance of the 'floor participant interface state transition' state machine is released. When an MCPTT call is released, the floor control server interface follows a two-step procedure: [Step 1]The floor control server interface stops sending floor control messages and RTP media packets to MCPTT clients in the MCPTT call; and [Step 2]When the application and signalling plane has determined that the MCPTT call has been released, resources in the floor control server interface are released, along with all 'floor participant interface state transition' state machines. The non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group can initiate an MCPTT call release depending on the release policy specified in 3GPP TS 24.379 .
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor control server interface procedures > General
Content: The floor control server interface is stateless with regards to the floor control message received and sent. The following clauses specifies what the floor control server interface shall do when receiving a floor control message sent by the controlling MCPTT function or received at the floor participant interface and how the floor control server controls the media distribution function in the non-controlling MCPTT function.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor control server interface procedures > Receiving a Floor Request message
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Request message from one floor participant interface, the floor control server interface: shall forward the Floor Request message to the controlling MCPTT function. The Floor Request message: shall include all fields included by the floor participant; if a Track Info field is included, shall include the temporary identifier at the end of the Floor Participant Reference value item; if a Track Info field is not included, shall include a Track Info field populated as follows: shall include the "mc_queueing" fmtp attribute value negotiated as specified in clause 14 in the Queueing Capability value; shall include a Participant Type value based on the participant-type element specified in 3GPP TS 24.481 , if value in the participant-type element is available, otherwise set the Participant Type value to "unknown"; and iii. shall include the temporary identifier as the first Floor Participant Reference value; and shall include the MCPTT ID of the requesting floor participant in a User ID field, if privacy is not requested; and if the value of the Queueing Capability in the Track Info is '1' (the floor participant in the MCPTT client supports queueing), shall store the outgoing Floor Request message in the passive floor request queue.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor control server interface procedures > Receive Floor Release message
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Release message from one floor participant interface, the floor control server interface: NOTE: A Floor Release message can be received from the permitted floor participant and from any participant that is queued in the floor control server. shall forward a Floor Release message to the controlling MCPTT function. The Floor Release message: shall include all fields included by the floor participant in the Floor Release message; if a Track Info field is included, shall include the temporary identifier at the end of the Floor Participant Reference value item; and if a Track Info field is not included, shall include a Track Info field as follows: shall include the "mc_queueing" fmtp attribute value negotiated as specified in clause 14 in the Queueing Capability value; and ii. shall include the temporary identifier as the first Floor Participant Reference value; and if a Floor Request message received from this floor participant is in the passive floor request queue, shall remove the floor request from the passive floor request queue.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor control server interface procedures > Receive Floor Queue Position Request message
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Queue Position Request message from one floor participant interface, the floor control server interface: shall forward the Floor Queue Position Request message to the controlling MCPTT function. The Floor Queue Position Request message: shall include all fields included by the floor participant; if a Track Info field is included, shall include the temporary identifier at the end of the Floor Participant Reference value item; and if a Track Info field is not included, shall include a Track Info field as follows: shall include the "mc_queueing" fmtp attribute value negotiated as specified in clause 14 in the Queueing Capability value; and ii. shall include the temporary identifier as the first Floor Participant Reference value.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor control server interface procedures > Receive Floor Ack message
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Ack message from one floor participant interface the floor control server interface: shall send the Floor Ack message towards the controlling MCPTT function. The Floor Ack message: shall include all fields included by the floor participant in the Floor Ack message; if Track Info field is included, shall include the temporary identifier at the end of the Floor Participant Reference value item; and if a Track Info field is not included, shall include a Track Info field with temporary identifier as the first Floor Participant Reference .
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor control server interface procedures > Receive Floor Granted message
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Granted message sent from the controlling MCPTT function, the floor control server interface: shall send the Floor Granted to the floor participant interface identified by the Participant Reference value at the end of the Track Info field.
