The Floor Granted message: shall include the fields as received with the following exceptions: if the Track Info field only contains one Participant Reference value, shall remove the Track Info field from the outgoing Floor Granted message; and if the Track Info field contains more than one Participant Reference value, shall remove the last Participant Reference value from the Track Info field from the outgoing Floor Granted message; and if the Floor Indicator field is included in the Floor Granted message and the G-bit is set to '1' (Dual floor), shall store the SSRC of granted floor participant and associate the stored value with dual floor; if: a SSRC of granted floor participant and associate the stored value with dual floor is not stored, shall send a Floor Taken message populated as specified in step d. below to all participant interfaces with the exception of the floor participant interface to which the Floor Granted message is sent; a SSRC of granted floor participant associate with dual floor is stored and if the Floor Indicator field is not included in the Floor Granted message or if the Floor Indicator field is included in the Floor Granted message and the G-bit is set to '0' (Not dual floor), shall send a Floor Taken message populated as specified in step d. below to all participant interfaces with the exception of: the floor participant interface to which the Floor Granted message is sent; and the floor participants only listening to the overriding floor participant; the Floor Indicator field is included in the Floor Granted message and the G-bit is set to '1' (Dual floor): shall send a Floor Taken message populated as specified in step d. below to floor participants that will listen to the RTP media from the overriding MCPTT client according to local policy; [NOTE 1:]The MCPTT client overridden by the overriding MCPTT client is still sending voice (overridden). The list of floor participants that receive the overriding, overridden, or both transmissions is based on configuration. The Floor Taken message: shall include the granted MCPTT user's MCPTT ID in the Granted Party's Identity field and may include the functional alias of the granted MCPTT user in the Functional Alias field, if privacy is not requested by the granted floor participant when the MCPTT client was invited to the session; [NOTE 2:]The privacy request was stored for each invited MCPTT client when the MCPTT client accepted the invitation as specified in clause 6.5.2. shall include in the Message Sequence Number field the local Message Sequence Number value increased with 1; shall include the Permission to Request Floor field to '0', if the group call is a broadcast group call; may include the Permission to Request the Floor field set to '1', if the group call is not a broadcast group call; and shall set the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Taken message to '0' (acknowledgement is not required); and [NOTE 3:]A Floor Taken message sent to all participants does not require acknowledgement. e. if the Floor Indicator field was included in the Floor Granted message, shall include the received Floor Indicator field; and if the Floor Request message received from the floor participant is in the passive floor request queue, shall remove the floor request from the passive floor request queue; and if the Floor Indicator field is included in the Floor Granted message and the G-bit is set to '1' (Dual floor), shall send a Floor Idle message to those floor participants that will only listen to RTP media from the overriding MCPTT client. The Floor Idle message: shall include the Floor Indicator field as received in the Floor Granted message with the G-bit set to '0' (Not dual floor); and shall include in the Message Sequence Number field the local Message Sequence Number value increased with 1.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor control server interface procedures > Receive Floor Deny message
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Deny message sent from the controlling MCPTT function, the floor control server interface: shall use the Participant Reference value at the end of the Track Info field to identify the floor participant interface; if: the Track Info field only contains one Participant Reference value, shall remove the Track Info field from the outgoing Floor Granted message; and if the Track Info field contains more than one Participant Reference value, shall remove the last Participant Reference value from the Track Info field; shall forward the Floor Deny message to the floor participant interface; and if the Floor Request message received from the floor participant is in the passive floor request queue, shall remove the floor request from the passive floor request queue.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor control server interface procedures > Receive Floor Idle message
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Idle message sent from the controlling MCPTT function, the floor control server interface: NOTE 1: The Floor Idle message can be either destined to floor participants in all MCPTT clients or is sent to the floor participant in a specific MCPTT client. In the latter case the Floor Idle message contains the Track Info field. if the Floor Idle message contains a Track Info field; shall use the Participant Reference value at the end of the Track Info field to identify the floor participant interface; if: the Track Info field only contains one Participant Reference value: shall remove the Track Info field from the outgoing Floor Idle message; shall increase the stored message sequence number value with 1; and shall include in the Message Sequence Number field the local Message Sequence Number value increased with 1; and if the Track Info field contains more than one Participant Reference value, shall remove the last Participant Reference value from the Track Info field; and shall send the Floor Idle message to the floor participant interface; if the Floor Idle message does not contain a Track Info field; shall set the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Idle message to '0' (acknowledgement is not required); NOTE 2: A Floor Idle message sent to all participants does not require acknowledgement. if: the Floor Indicator field is not included in the Floor Idle message or if the Floor Indicator field is included and the G-bit is set to '0' (Not dual floor), shall send the Floor Idle message to all floor participant interfaces. The Floor Idle message: shall include received fields; and shall include in the Message Sequence Number field the local Message Sequence Number value increased with 1; and the Floor Indicator field is included in the Floor Idle message and the G-bit is set to '1' (Dual floor), shall send the Floor Idle message to floor participants listening to the overriding MCPTT client according to local policy. The Floor Idle message: shall include received fields; and shall include in the Message Sequence Number field the local Message Sequence Number value increased with 1; and shall discard the stored SSRC of granted floor participant associated with a dual floor; shall send a Floor Ack message towards the controlling MCPTT function if the first bit in the subtype of the received Floor Idle message is set to '1' (acknowledgement is required) as specified in clause 8.2.2. The Floor Ack message: shall include the Source field set to '3' (the non-controlling MCPTT function is the source); and shall include the Message Type field set to '5' (Floor Idle); and 3. shall empty the passive floor request queue.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor control server interface procedures > Receive Floor Taken message
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Taken message sent from the controlling MCPTT function, the floor control server interface: NOTE 1: The Floor Taken message can be either destined to floor participants in all MCPTT clients or is sent to the floor participant in a specific MCPTT client. In the latter case the Floor Taken message contains the Track Info field. if the Floor Taken message contains a Track Info field; shall use the Participant Reference value at the end of the Track Info field to identify the floor participant interface; if the Track Info field only contains one Participant Reference value: shall remove the Track Info field from the outgoing Floor Taken message; and shall include in the Message Sequence Number field the local Message Sequence Number value increased with 1; if the Track Info field contains more than one Participant Reference value, shall remove the last Participant Reference value from the Track Info field; and shall send the Floor Taken message to the floor participant interface; if the Floor Taken message does not contain a Track Info field: shall set the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Taken message to '0' (acknowledgement is not required); NOTE 2: A Floor Taken message sent to all participants does not require acknowledgement. if the Floor Indicator field is included in the Floor Taken message and the G-bit is set to '1' (Dual floor), shall store the SSRC of granted floor participant and associate the stored SSRC with dual floor; if there is no stored SSRC of granted floor participant associated with a dual floor, shall send the Floor Taken message to all floor participant interfaces. The Floor Taken message: shall include all received fields with the exception of the Message Sequence Number field; shall include in the Message Sequence Number field the local Message Sequence Number value increased with 1; if there is a stored SSRC of granted floor participant associated with a dual floor: if the Floor Indicator field is not included in the Floor Taken message or if the Floor Indicator field is included in the Floor Taken message and the G-bit is set to '0' (Not dual floor), shall send the Floor Taken message to all floor participant interfaces with the exception of the floor participants only listening to RTP media from the overriding floor participant. The Floor Idle message: shall include all received fields; and shall include in the Message Sequence Number field the local Message Sequence Number value increased with 1; and if the Floor Indicator field is included in the Floor Taken message and the G-bit is set to '1' (Dual floor): shall send the Floor Taken message to all floor participants listening to the RTP media from the overriding MCPTT client according to local policy. The Floor Taken message: shall include all received fields; and shall include in the Message Sequence Number field the local Message Sequence Number value increased with 1; and shall send a Floor Idle message to those floor participants that will only listen to RTP media from the overriding MCPTT client according to local policy. The Floor Idle message: shall include all received fields; shall include in the Message Sequence Number field the local Message Sequence Number value increased with 1; and shall update the received Floor Indicator field and set the G-bit to '0' and; shall send a Floor Ack message towards the controlling MCPTT function if the first bit in the subtype of the received Floor Taken message is set to '1' (acknowledgement is required) as specified in clause 8.2.2. The Floor Ack message: shall include the Source field set to '3' (the non-controlling MCPTT function is the source); and shall include the Message Type field set to '2' (Floor Taken);
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor control server interface procedures > Receive Floor Revoke message
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Revoke message from the controlling MCPTT function, the floor control server interface: shall use the Participant Reference value at the end of the Track Info field to identify the floor participant interface; if: the Track Info field only contains one Participant Reference value, shall remove the Track Info field from the outgoing Floor Revoke message; and if the Track Info field contains more than one Participant Reference value, shall remove the last Participant Reference value from the Track Info field; and shall forward the Floor Revoke message to the floor participant interface.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor control server interface procedures > Receive Floor Queue Position Info message
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Queue Position Info message from the controlling MCPTT function, the floor control server interface: shall use the Participant Reference value at the end of the Track Info field to identify the floor participant interface; if: the Track Info field only contains one Participant Reference value, shall remove the Track Info field from the outgoing Floor Granted message; and if the Track Info field contains more than one Participant Reference value, shall remove the last Participant Reference value from the Track Info field; and shall forward the Floor Queue Position Info message to the floor participant interface.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor control server interface procedures > Receive RTP media packets from controlling MCPTT function
Content: Upon receiving an indication from the media distributor that RTP media packets are received from the controlling MCPTT function, the floor control server interface: 1. if a SSRC of granted floor participant associated with a dual floor is not stored shall request the network media distributor to forward received RTP media packets to all MCPTT clients in the session controlled by the non-controlling MCPTT function where the SSRC of the received RTP media packets are different to SSRC used by an MCPTT client; NOTE: If one of the MCPTT client controlled by the non-controlling MCPTT function is granted the floor, media originated from that MCPTT client is not distributed back to the MCPTT client granted the floor. if a SSRC of granted floor participant associated with a dual floor is stored and if the SSRC in the media packet is not the same as the stored SSRC, shall request the network media distributor to forward received RTP media packets to MCPTT clients controlled by the non-controlling MCPTT function: where the SSRC of the received RTP media packets are different to SSRC used by the MCPTT client; and that are not only listening to the dual floor according to local policy; and if a SSRC of granted floor participant associated with a dual floor is stored and if the SSRC in the media packet is the same as the stored SSRC, shall request the network media distributor to forward received RTP media packets to all MCPTT client: where the SSRC of the received RTP media packets are different to SSRC used by the MCPTT client; and listening to the dual floor according to local policy.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor control server interface procedures > Receive RTP media packets from an MCPTT client
Content: Upon receiving an indication from the media distribution function that RTP media packets are received from one of the network media interfaces, the floor control server interface: 1. shall request the network media distributor to forward received RTP media packets towards the controlling MCPTT function. NOTE: If RTP media packets are received from an MCPTT client not permitted to send media, the floor participant interface will send a Floor Revoke message to the floor participant of the misbehaving MCPTT client without involving the floor control server interface.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor control server interface procedures > MCPTT session release step 1
Content: Upon receiving an MCPTT call release step 1 request from the application and signalling plane e.g. when the session is going to be released, the floor control interface: shall ignore floor control message from the floor control server; shall request the media distributor to stop distributing RTP media packets to the network media interface of the MCPTT clients; and shall ignore any floor control messages received from the floor participant interfaces.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor control server interface procedures > MCPTT session release step 2
Content: Upon receiving an MCPTT call release step 2 request from the application and signalling plane, the floor control server interface: 1. shall release all resources associated with this session.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor control server interface procedures > Receiving a split instruction (R: Split)
Content: Upon receiving an instruction from the application and signalling plane to split the ongoing group session, as specified in 3GPP TS 24.379 in clause 10.1.1.5.2.4 for prearranged group call and in clause 10.1.2.5.1.4 for chat group call, the floor control server interface: 1. shall perform the actions in clause 6.3.2.3.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor control server interface procedures > Receive Floor Release Multi Talker message
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Release Multi Talker message sent from the controlling MCPTT function, the floor control server interface: shall use the Participant Reference value at the end of the Track Info field to identify the floor participant interface; if: the Track Info field only contains one Participant Reference value, shall remove the Track Info field from the outgoing Floor Release Multi Talker message; and the Track Info field contains more than one Participant Reference value, shall remove the last Participant Reference value from the Track Info field; and shall forward the Floor Release Multi Talker message to the floor participant interface.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor participant interface procedures > General
Content: The floor participant interface shall behave according to the state diagram and state transitions specified in this clause. Figure 6.5.5.1-1 shows the general floor control operation states (P states) and the state transition diagram. The floor participant interface shall keep one instance of the 'floor participant interface state transition' state machine per MCPTT client in a session. The floor participant associated to the 'floor participant interface state transition' state machine is in the following clauses referred to as the associated floor participant. If floor control messages or RTP media packets arrives in a state where there is no procedure specified in the following clauses the floor participant interface: shall discard the floor control message; shall request the network media interface to discard any received RTP media packet; and shall remain in the current state. State details are explained in the following clauses.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor participant interface procedures > State: 'Start-Stop' > General
Content: When a new instance of the 'Floor participant interface state transition' state machine is initiated, before any floor control related input is applied, the state machine is in 'Start-stop' state. Similarly when the session is released the state machine shall return to the 'Start-stop' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor participant interface procedures > State: 'Start-Stop' > Participant invited to session
Content: When the floor participant interface receives an indication from the floor control server interface that an MCPTT client has accepted the invitation to a session (i.e. when the SIP 200 (OK) response to the initial SIP INVITE request is received as specified in 3GPP TS 24.379 ), the floor participant interface: 1. shall enter the 'P: has no permission' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor participant interface procedures > State: 'P: has no permission' > General
Content: The floor participant interface uses this state when the associated floor participant is not permitted to send media.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor participant interface procedures > State: 'P: has no permission' > Receive Floor Idle message (R: Floor Idle)
Content: When the floor participant interface receives a Floor Idle message from the floor control server interface, the floor participant interface: shall send the Floor Idle message to the floor participant; if the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Idle message is set to '1' (acknowledgement is required) as specified in clause 8.2.2, shall store an indication that a Floor Ack message to a Floor Idle messages is expected; and shall remain in the 'P: has no permission' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor participant interface procedures > State: 'P: has no permission' > Receive Floor Taken message (R: Floor Taken)
Content: When the floor participant interface receives a Floor Taken message from the floor control server interface, the floor participant interface: shall send the Floor Taken message to the floor participant; if the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Taken message is set to '1' (acknowledgement is required) as specified in clause 8.2.2, shall store an indication that a Floor Ack message to a Floor Taken messages is expected; and shall remain in the 'P: has no permission' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor participant interface procedures > State: 'P: has no permission' > Receive Floor Request message (R: Floor Request)
Content: When the floor participant interface receives a Floor Request message from the floor participant, the floor participant interface: shall send the Floor Request message to the floor control server interface; and shall remain in the 'P: has no permission' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor participant interface procedures > State: 'P: has no permission' > Receive Floor Granted message (R: Floor Granted)
Content: When the floor participant interface receives a Floor Granted message from the floor control server interface, the floor participant interface: shall send the Floor Granted message to the floor participant; if the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Granted message is set to '1' (acknowledgement is required) as specified in clause 8.2.2, shall store an indication that a Floor Ack message to a Floor Granted messages is expected; and shall enter the 'P: has permission' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor participant interface procedures > State: 'P: has no permission' > Receive Floor Deny message (R: Floor Deny)
Content: When the floor participant interface receives a Floor Deny message from the floor control server interface, the floor participant interface: shall send the Floor Deny message to the floor participant; if the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Deny message is set to '1' (acknowledgement is required) as specified in clause 8.2.2, shall store an indication that a Floor Ack message to a Floor Deny messages is expected; and shall remain in the 'P: has no permission' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor participant interface procedures > State: 'P: has no permission' > Receive Floor Queue Position Info message (R: Floor Queue Position Info)
Content: When the floor participant interface receives a Floor Queue Position Info message from the floor control server interface, the floor participant interface: shall send the Floor Queue Position Info message to the floor participant; if the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Queue Position Info message is set to '1' (acknowledgement is required) as specified in clause 8.2.2, shall store an indication that a Floor Ack message to a Floor Queue Position Info messages is expected; and shall remain in the 'P: has no permission' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor participant interface procedures > State: 'P: has no permission' > Receive Floor Queue Position Request message (R: Floor Queue Position Request)
Content: When the floor participant interface receives a Floor Queue Position Request message from the floor participant, the floor participant interface: shall send the Floor Queue Position Request message to the floor control server interface; and shall remain in the 'P: has no permission' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor participant interface procedures > State: 'P: has no permission' > Receive RTP media packets (R: RTP media)
Content: When the floor participant interface receives an indication from the network media interface that RTP media packets are received from the media distributor, the floor participant interface shall instruct the network media interface to send the received RTP media packets towards the MCPTT client; and shall remain in the 'P: has no permission' state. When the floor participant interface receives an indication from the network media interface that RTP media packets are received from the MCPTT client, the floor participant interface shall send a Floor Revoke message to the floor participant. The Floor Revoke message: shall include the Reject Cause field with the Reject Cause value set to \#3 (No permission to send a Media Burst); shall store that a Floor Release message is expected from the floor participant; and shall remain in the 'P: has no permission' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor participant interface procedures > State: 'P: has no permission' > Receive Floor Release message (R: Floor Release)
Content: When the floor participant interface receives a Floor Release message from the floor participant, the floor participant interface: if a Floor Release message is not expected from the floor participant: if the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Release message is set to '1' (acknowledgement is required) as specified in clause 8.2.2, based on local policy: shall send a Floor Ack message to the floor participant and set the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Release message to '0' (acknowledgement is not required) in the outgoing Floor Release message; or wait for the Floor Ack from the floor control server; and shall forward the Floor Release message to the floor control server interface; if a Floor Release message is expected from the floor participant: if the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Release message is set to '1' (acknowledgement is required) as specified in clause 8.2.2: shall send a Floor Ack message to the floor participant; and shall remove that a Floor Release message is expected from the floor participant; and shall remain in the 'P: has no permission' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor participant interface procedures > State: 'P: has no permission' > Receive split instruction (R: Split)
Content: Upon receiving an instruction to split the ongoing MCPTT call, to the floor participant interface: shall create a new instance of the 'basic floor control operation towards the floor participant' state machine; shall move information associated with the instance used for 'floor participant interface state transition' to the 'basic floor control operation towards the floor participant' state machine; NOTE: Which information that needs to be moved is an implementation option. shall enter the 'Start-stop' state and terminate the 'floor participant interface state transition' state machine associated with this floor participant and this session; if the state in 'general floor control operation' state machine is 'G: Floor Idle' state; shall enter the 'U: not permitted and Floor Idle' state as specified in clause 6.3.5.3.2; and if the state in 'general floor control operation' state machine is 'G: Floor Taken' state; shall enter the 'U: not permitted and Floor Taken' state as specified in clause 6.3.5.4.2.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor participant interface procedures > State: 'P: has no permission' > Receive Floor Release Multi Talker message
Content: When the floor participant interface receives a Floor Release Multi Talker message from the floor control server interface, the floor participant interface: shall send the Floor Release Multi Talker message to the floor participant; and shall remain in the 'P: has no permission' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor participant interface procedures > State: 'P: has permission' > General
Content: The floor participant interface uses this state when the floor participant has permission to send media
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor participant interface procedures > State: 'P: has permission' > Receive RTP media packets
Content: When the floor participant interface receives an indication from the network media interface that RTP media packets are received from the MCPTT client, the floor participant interface: shall instruct the media interface to forward received RTP media packets towards the media distributor; and shall remain in the 'P: has permission' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor participant interface procedures > State: 'P: has permission' > Receive Floor Release message
Content: When the floor participant interface receives a Floor Release message from the floor participant, the floor participant interface: shall send the Floor Release message to the floor control server interface; and shall remain in the 'P: has permission' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor participant interface procedures > State: 'P: has permission' > Receive Floor Ack message
Content: When the floor participant interface receives a Floor Ack message from the floor control server interface, the floor participant interface: shall send the Floor Ack message to the floor participant; and shall remain in the 'P: has permission' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor participant interface procedures > State: 'P: has permission' > Receive Floor Idle message
Content: When the floor participant interface receives a Floor Idle message from the floor control server interface, the floor participant interface: shall send the Floor Idle message to the floor participant; if the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Idle message is set to '1' (acknowledgement is required), shall store an indication that a Floor Ack message to a Floor Idle messages is expected; and shall enter the 'P: has no permission' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor participant interface procedures > State: 'P: has permission' > Receive Floor Taken message
Content: When the floor participant interface receives a Floor Taken message from the floor control server interface, the floor participant interface: shall send the Floor Taken message to the floor participant; if the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Taken message is set to '1' (acknowledgement is required), shall store an indication that a Floor Ack message to a Floor Taken messages is expected; and if the Floor Indicator field is included and the I-bit is set to '1' (multi-talker) shall remain in the 'P: has permission' state, otherwise shall enter the 'P: has no permission' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor participant interface procedures > State: 'P: has permission' > Receive Floor Revoke message
Content: When the floor participant interface receives a Floor Revoke message from the floor control server interface, the floor participant interface: shall send the Floor Revoke message to the floor participant; if the first bit in the subtype of the Floor Revoke message is set to '1' (acknowledgement is required), shall store an indication that a Floor Ack message to a Floor Revoke messages is expected; and shall remain in the 'P: has permission' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor participant interface procedures > State: 'P: has permission' > Receive split instruction (R: Split)
Content: Upon receiving an instruction to split the ongoing MCPTT call, the floor participant interface: shall create a new instance of the 'basic floor control operation towards the floor participant' state machine as specified in clause 6.3.5; shall move information associated with the instance used for 'floor participant interface state transition' to the 'basic floor control operation towards the floor participant' state machine; NOTE: Which information that needs to be moved is an implementation option. shall enter the 'Start-stop' state and terminate the 'floor participant interface state transition' state machine associated with this floor participant and this session; and shall enter the 'U: permitted' state as specified in clause 6.3.5.5.2.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor participant interface procedures > State: 'P: has permission' > Receive Floor Release Multi Talker message
Content: When the floor participant interface receives a Floor Release Multi Talker message from the floor control server interface, the floor participant interface: shall send the Floor Release Multi Talker message to the floor participant; and shall remain in the 'P: has permission' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor participant interface procedures > In any state > General
Content: This clause describes the actions to be taken in all states defined for the 'floor participant interface state transition' diagram with the exception of the 'Start-stop' and 'Releasing' states.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor participant interface procedures > In any state > Receive Floor Ack message (R: Floor Ack)
Content: If a Floor Ack message is received from the floor participant, the floor participant interface: if an indication exists that a Floor Ack message is expected for the message in the Message Type field; shall forward the Floor Ack message to the floor control server interface; and shall remove the indication that a Floor Ack message is expected for the message in the Message Type field; and [NOTE:]It is an implementation option what action to take if an indication exists that a Floor Ack message is expected for the message in the Message Type field, but the Floor Ack message is not received 2. shall remain in the current state. If a Floor Ack message is received from the floor control server interface, the floor participant interface: shall send the Floor Ack message to the floor participant; and shall remain in the current state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor participant interface procedures > In any state > MCPTT session release step 1 (MCPTT call release - 1)
Content: Upon receiving an MCPTT call release step 1 request from the application and signalling plane e.g. when the session is going to be released or when the MCPTT client leaves the session, the floor participant interface: shall stop sending floor control messages to the floor participant; shall request the network media interface to stop sending RTP media packets towards to the MCPTT client; shall ignore any floor control messages received from the floor participant; shall request the network media interface to stop forwarding RTP media packets from the MCPTT client to the media distributor; shall indicate to the floor control server interface that the MCPTT client has started to disconnect from the session; and shall enter the 'P: Releasing' state.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor participant interface procedures > State: 'P: Releasing' > General
Content: The floor participant interface uses this state while waiting for the application and signalling plane to finalize the release of the session or finalizing the removal of the MCPTT client from the session.
Title Path: On-network floor control > Non-controlling MCPTT function of an MCPTT group > Floor participant interface procedures > State: 'P: Releasing' > MCPTT session release step 2 (MCPTT call release - 2)
Content: Upon receiving an MCPTT call release step 2 request from the application and signalling plane, the floor participant interface: shall request the network media interface to release all resources associated with this MCPTT client for this MCPTT call; and shall enter the 'Start-stop' state and terminate the 'floor participant interface state transition' state machine associated with this floor participant and this session.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > General
Content: A floor control session may be initiated only if there is a successfully established off-network call. In off-network, floor control is performed using floor control messages among the MCPTT clients without a centralized floor arbitrator. When off-network, if a floor control session is active, the floor arbitrator and the floor participant are co-located in the MCPTT client (see 3GPP TS 23.379 ). During a floor control session the MCPTT client currently speaking serves as the temporary floor arbitrator. All other MCPTT clients in the call play the role of floor participant. When the floor arbitrator grants the floor to another MCPTT client, that new MCPTT client, when starts to send media, becomes the new floor arbitrator and the former (the MCPTT client which granted the floor) becomes a floor participant. The procedures in clause 7.2 are from the perspective of a single MCPTT client. No special message other than floor control messages and media is used for coordinating the floor arbitrator and floor participant status of the separate MCPTT clients participating in the off-network call. The floor control messages are always sent to all the participants of the call. Therefore they can be monitored by any MCPTT client listening to the call. In a floor control session queueing of floor requests may be supported. It is assumed that the MCPTT user presses the PTT button for requesting talk permission and keeps it pressed until the request is resolved. If queueing of floor requests is not supported, this request is either granted or rejected or no answer is received. If the request is granted the user is notified with talk permission tone (or equivalent) and the user continues to press the PTT until the user finishes the talk burst. If the request is rejected or no answer is received the user is notified and releases the PTT button. If queueing of floor requests is supported, the MCPTT user shall be notified when a floor request is queued and the MCPTT user shall release the PTT button. When, after queueing, the floor is granted to this user the MCPTT user shall be informed that the queued request is now granted. Then the MCPTT user should press the PTT button within a short duration. Otherwise, the grant is taken from this MCPTT user. An MCPTT user can appear in a queue only once. The floor request queue is transferred from the former to the new floor arbitrator. After the initiation of a floor control session the MCPTT client behaves according to the state machine presented in clause 7.2.3. The state machine is designed such that in normal cases only one of the MCPTT clients, which participates in the call, acts as floor arbitrator and all others act as floor participants. However, there may be situations such that more than one MCPTT client are at an internal state causing them to act as floor arbitrator. A short sequence of floor control messages and RTP media packets are initiated to resolve these situations.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant procedures at MCPTT session initialization
Content: This clause applies when no active floor control session exists. Before a floor control entity is initiated a state machine with a single state, named as 'Start-stop' state, shall exist. At 'Start-stop' state, when the MCPTT client receives a request of the MCPTT call control entity to initiate the floor control as originating client, then the MCPTT client shall initiate a floor control entity and the floor control entity shall enter into the 'O: has permission' state. Otherwise, if MCPTT client receives a request of the MCPTT call control entity to initiate the floor control as terminating client, then the MCPTT client shall initiate a floor control entity and the floor control entity for an MCPTT group call shall enter into the 'O: silence' state or for both MCPTT private call and MCPTT broadcast group call shall enter the 'O: has no permission' state. Once the session is initiated, the initial floor control messages are sent according to the state machine presented in clause 7.2.3. Normally, once the session is started the originating MCPTT client has the floor implicitly. For an on-going off-network group call, if an MCPTT client joins later, then it starts the floor control session and takes the role of floor participant and enters 'O: silence' state.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant procedures at MCPTT session initialization > Determine off-network floor priority
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Request message, to determine the floor priority of the Floor Request message, the floor arbitrator: shall check the presence of Floor Priority field in the received Floor Request message. If present, the floor arbitrator: shall determine the floor priority of the Floor Request message by choosing the lowest value from the following inputs: the value of the Floor Priority field in the received Floor Request message; the value of the "/ x / x /Common/MCPTTGroupMemberList/ x /UserPriority" leaf node of the sender of the Floor Request message, present in group configuration as specified in 3GPP TS 24.483 ; and the value of the "/ x /OffNetwork/NumLevelHierarchy" leaf node present in service configuration as specified in 3GPP TS 24.483 ; and if the Floor Priority field is not present in the Floor Request message, the floor arbitrator: shall use the minimum value allowed for the Floor Priority as floor priority of the Floor Request message. Once the floor priority of the Floor Request message is determined, to determine the effective priority of the Floor Request message, the floor arbitrator: shall check the type of call indicated by the Floor Indicator field of the received Floor Request message and: if the type of call indicated by the Floor Indicator field is Normal call: if the current type of the call is normal, shall continue to check the next input parameter from step 2; and if the current type of the call is emergency or imminent-peril and: if the value of "/ x / x /OffNetwork/QueueUsage" leaf node present in the group configuration as specified in 3GPP TS 24.483 is set to "true", shall queue the floor request; if the value of "/ x / x /OffNetwork/QueueUsage" leaf node present in the group configuration as specified in 3GPP TS 24.483 is set to "false", shall deny the floor request; and shall skip step 2; if the type of call indicated by the Floor Indicator field is Imminent peril call and: if the current type of the call is normal, shall pre-empt the current talker, grant the floor request and skip step 2; if the current type of the call is imminent-peril, shall continue to check the next input parameter from step 2; and if the current type of the call is emergency and: if the value of "/ x / x /OffNetwork/QueueUsage" leaf node present in the group configuration as specified in 3GPP TS 24.483 is set to "true", shall queue the floor request; if the value of "/ x / x /OffNetwork/QueueUsage" leaf node present in the group configuration as specified in 3GPP TS 24.483 is set to "false", shall deny the floor request; and shall skip step 2; and if the type of the call indicated by the Floor Indicator field is Emergency call and: if the current type of the call is normal or imminent-peril, shall pre-empt the current talker and grant the floor request; and if the current type of the call is emergency and: if the value of "/ x / x /OffNetwork/QueueUsage" leaf node present in the group configuration as specified in 3GPP TS 24.483 is set to "true", shall queue the floor request; if the value of "/ x / x /OffNetwork/QueueUsage" leaf node present in the group configuration as specified in 3GPP TS 24.483 is set to "false", shall deny the floor request; and shall skip step 2; and shall compare the determined floor priority of the received Floor Request message to the effective priority of the current talker (determined at the time of floor grant to the current talker) and: if the effective priority of the current talker is equal to or higher than the determined floor priority of the Floor Request message and: if the value of "/ x / x /OffNetwork/QueueUsage" leaf node present in the group configuration as specified in 3GPP TS 24.483 is set to "true", shall queue the floor request; and if the value of "/ x / x /OffNetwork/QueueUsage" leaf node present in the group configuration as specified in 3GPP TS 24.483 is set to "false", shall deny the floor request; and if the determined floor priority of the Floor Request message is higher than that of the current talker, shall pre-empt the current talker and shall grant the floor request.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant procedures at MCPTT call release
Content: This clause applies when an active floor control session exists. When the off-network call is released the floor control session is terminated. The off-network floor control session can also be terminated when no media transmission or reception takes place during floor control session hold time, T230 (Inactivity). The termination of the floor control session as a result of the expiry of timer T230 (Inactivity) may terminate the call session.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > General
Content: The floor participant shall behave according to the state diagram and the transitions specified in this clause. The received floor messages and the RTP media packets are inputs to the state machine according to their arrival order. They are not ignored unless otherwise stated. The MCPTT client also provides input to the state machine as request to talk (press PTT button) or as end of talk (release PTT button). Figure 7.2.3.1-1 show the 'Floor participant state diagram basic operation'. State details are explained in the following clauses. If an RTP media packet or a floor control message arrives in a state where there is no specific procedure specified for the RTP media packet or the received floor control message, the floor participant shall discard the floor control message or the RTP media packet and shall remain in the current state. NOTE: A badly formatted RTP packet or floor control message received in any state is ignored by the floor participant and does not cause any change of the current state.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'Start-stop' > General
Content: When a new instance of the state machine is created, before any floor control related input is applied, the state machine is in the 'Start-stop' state. Similarly when the call is released or the floor control session is terminated, the state machine shall return to the 'Start-stop' state.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'Start-stop' > MCPTT call established originating MCPTT user
Content: When an MCPTT call is established with session announcement including an explicit floor request, the originating floor participant: shall create an instance of a floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation state machine; shall send Floor Granted message towards other floor participants. The Floor Granted message: shall include the granted priority in the Floor priority field; shall include the MCPTT user's own MCPTT ID in the User ID field; and if the floor request is a broadcast group call, system call, emergency call or an imminent peril call, shall include a Floor Indicator field indicating the relevant call types; shall set the stored SSRC of the current florr arbitrator to its own SSRC; and shall enter 'O: has permission' state.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'Start-stop' > MCPTT group call established terminating MCPTT user
Content: When an MCPTT call is established the terminating floor participant: shall create an instance of a floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation state machine; shall start timer T230 (Inactivity); and shall enter 'O: silence' state.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'Start-stop' > MCPTT private call established terminating MCPTT user
Content: When an MCPTT private call is established the terminating floor participant: shall create an instance of a floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation state machine; shall start timer T203(End of RTP media); and shall enter 'O: has no permission' state.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'Start-stop' > Send Floor Request message (PTT button pressed)
Content: If the floor participant receives an indication from the MCPTT user to send media, the floor participant: shall create an instance of a floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation state machine; shall send the Floor Request message to other floor participants. The Floor Request message: if a different priority than the normal priority is required, shall include the Floor Priority field with the requested priority in the Floor Priority value; shall include the MCPTT ID of the MCPTT user in the User ID value of the User ID field; and if the floor request is a broadcast group call, system call, emergency call or an imminent peril call, shall include a Floor Indicator field indicating the relevant call types; shall initialize the counter C201 (Floor request) with value set to 1; shall start the timer T201 (Floor request); and shall enter 'O: pending request' state.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'Start-stop' > Receive Floor Taken message (R: Floor Taken)
Content: When a Floor Taken message is received, the floor participant: shall create an instance of a floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation state machine; may provide a floor taken notification to the MCPTT user; shall set the stored SSRC of the current floor arbitrator to the SSRC of granted floor participant field in the Floor Taken message; shall start timer T203 (End of RTP media); and shall enter 'O: has no permission' state.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'Start-stop' > Receive Floor Granted message (R: Floor Granted to other)
Content: When a Floor Granted message is received and if the User ID in the Floor Granted message does not match its own User ID, the floor participant: shall create an instance of a floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation state machine; may provide a floor taken notification to the MCPTT user; shall set the stored SSRC of the candidate floor arbitrator to the SSRC of user to whom the floor was granted in the Floor Granted message; shall start timer T203 (End of RTP media); and shall enter 'O: has no permission' state.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'Start-stop' > Receive RTP media (R: RTP media)
Content: Upon receiving RTP media packets, the floor participant: shall create an instance of a floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation state machine; may provide a floor taken notification to the MCPTT user; shall set the stored SSRC of the current floor arbitrator to the SSRC of RTP media packet; shall restart timer T203 (End of RTP media); shall request the MCPTT client to start rendering received RTP media packets; and shall enter 'O: has no permission' state.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'Start-stop' > MCPTT broadcast group call established terminating MCPTT user
Content: When an MCPTT broadcast group call is established the terminating floor participant: shall create an instance of a floor participant state transition diagram for basic operation state machine; shall start timer T203 (End of RTP media); and shall enter 'O: has no permission' state. NOTE: In MCPTT broadcast group call, only originating MCPTT user is allowed to request floor and transmit media. A Floor Request message is locally denied to terminating MCPTT user, if requested.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'O: silence' > General
Content: When in this state the MCPTT client for the session is unaware of any MCPTT client acting as a floor arbitrator, has not itself initiated a floor control request and is not currently receiving RTP media packets. Timer T230 (Inactivity) is running in this state.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'O: silence' > Send Floor Request message (PTT button pressed)
Content: If the floor participant receives an indication from the MCPTT user to send media, the floor participant: shall send the Floor Request message to other floor participants. The Floor Request message: if a priority different than the default floor priority is required, shall include the Floor Priority field with the requested priority in the Floor Priority element; shall include the MCPTT ID of the MCPTT user in the User ID value of the User ID field; and if the floor request is a broadcast group call, system call, emergency call or an imminent peril call, shall include a Floor Indicator field indicating the relevant call types; shall initialize the counter C201 (Floor request) with value set to 1; shall stop timer T230 (Inactivity); shall start timer T201 (Floor Request); and shall enter 'O: pending request' state.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'O: silence' > Receive RTP media (R: RTP media)
Content: Upon receiving RTP media packets and if there is no stored SSRC of the current floor arbitrator, the floor participant: may provide a floor taken notification to the MCPTT user; shall stop timer T230 (Inactivity); shall set the stored SSRC of the current floor arbitrator to the SSRC of RTP media packet; shall restart (or start, if not running already) timer T203 (End of RTP media); shall request the MCPTT client to start rendering received RTP media packets; and shall enter 'O: has no permission' state. Otherwise, if SSRC of floor participant sending the media matches the stored SSRC of current floor arbitrator, the floor participant: shall restart (or start, if not running already) timer T203 (End of RTP media); shall stop timer T230 (Inactivity); shall request the MCPTT client to start rendering received RTP media packets; and shall enter 'O: has no permission' state.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'O: silence' > Receive Floor Granted message (R: Floor Granted to other)
Content: When a Floor Granted message is received and if the User ID in the Floor Granted message does not match its own User ID, the floor participant: may provide a floor taken notification to the MCPTT user; if the Floor Indicator field is included and the B-bit is set to '1' (Broadcast group call), shall provide a notification to the user indicating that this is a broadcast group call; shall set the stored SSRC of the candidate floor arbitrator to the SSRC of user to whom the floor was granted in the Floor Granted message; shall stop timer T230 (Inactivity); shall start timer T203 (End of RTP media); and shall enter 'O: has no permission' state.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'O: silence' > Receive Floor Request message (R: Floor Request)
Content: The transition is used in private call only. When a Floor Request message is received, the floor participant: shall send a Floor Granted message toward the other floor participant. The Floor Granted message: shall include the MCPTT ID of the Floor Request message received in User ID value of the User ID field; shall include the SSRC of the Floor Request message received in the SSRC of floor control server field; shall include the max duration as configured in the MCPTT client in the OffNetwork/MaxDuration parameter in the Duration value of the Duration field; and shall include the priority of the Floor Request message received in the Floor Priority value of the Floor Priority field; shall stop timer T230 (Inactivity); shall start timer T205 (Floor Granted ); and shall enter 'O: pending granted' state.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'O: silence' > Receive Floor Taken message (R: Floor Taken)
Content: When a Floor Taken message is received, the floor participant: may provide a floor taken notification to the MCPTT user; shall set the stored SSRC of the current floor arbitrator to the SSRC of granted floor participant field in the Floor Taken message; shall stop timer T230 (Inactivity); shall start timer T203 (End of RTP media); and shall enter 'O: has no permission' state.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'O: silence' > Timer T230 (Inactivity) expired
Content: Upon expiry of timer T230 (Inactivity), the floor participant: shall indicate to the call control that timer T230 (inactivity) has expired; shall terminate the instance of floor participant state transition diagram; and shall enter 'Start-stop' state.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'O: has no permission' > General
Content: In this state the MCPTT client does not have permission to send media.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'O: has no permission' > Sending Floor Request message (PTT button pressed)
Content: If the floor participant receives an indication from the MCPTT user that the MCPTT user wants to send media, the floor participant: shall send the Floor Request message to other clients. The Floor Request message: if a priority different than the default floor priority is required, shall include the Floor Priority field with the requested priority in the Floor Priority element; shall include the MCPTT ID of the MCPTT user in the User ID field; and if the floor request is a broadcast group call, system call, emergency call or an imminent peril call, shall include a Floor Indicator field indicating the relevant call types; shall initialize the counter C201 (Floor request) with value set to 1; shall start timer T201 (Floor Request); and shall enter 'O: pending request' state.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'O: has no permission' > Receive Floor Release message (R: Floor Release)
Content: When a Floor Release message is received and if the SSRC in the Floor Release message matches with the stored SSRC of the current arbitrator or with the stored SSRC of the candidate arbitrator, the floor participant: may provide floor idle notification to the MCPTT user. shall request the MCPTT client to stop rendering received RTP media packets; shall stop timer T203 (End of RTP media); shall start timer T230 (Inactivity); shall clear the stored SSRC of the candidate arbitrator; shall clear the stored SSRC of the current floor arbitrator; and shall enter 'O: silence' state;
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'O: has no permission' > Timer T203 (End of RTP media) expired
Content: On expiry of T203 (End of RTP media) timer, the floor participant: may provide floor idle notification to the MCPTT user. shall request the MCPTT client to stop rendering received RTP media packets; shall start timer T230 (Inactivity); shall clear the stored SSRC of the current floor arbitrator; and shall enter 'O: silence' state;
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'O: has no permission' > Receive Floor Granted message (R: Floor Granted to other)
Content: When a Floor Granted message is received and if the User ID value in the User ID field does not match its own MCPTT ID and SSRC of floor participant sending the Floor Granted message matches the stored SSRC of current floor arbitrator, the floor participant: shall request the MCPTT client to stop rendering received RTP media packets; shall restart timer T203 (End of RTP media); shall set the stored SSRC of the candidate floor arbitrator to the SSRC of user to whom the floor was granted in the Floor Granted message; may provide a floor taken notification to the MCPTT user; if the Floor Indicator field is included with the B-bit set to '1' (Broadcast group call), shall provide a notification to the user indicating that this is a broadcast group call; and shall remain in the 'O: has no permission' state.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'O: has no permission' > Receive RTP media (R: RTP media)
Content: Upon receiving RTP media packets and if there is no stored SSRC of the current floor arbitrator, the floor participant: shall request the MCPTT client to render the received RTP media packets; shall set the stored SSRC of the current floor arbitrator to the SSRC of RTP media packet; shall clear the stored SSRC of the candidate arbitrator, if any; shall restart timer T203 (End of RTP media); and shall remain in 'O: has no permission' state. Otherwise, if SSRC of floor participant sending the media matches the stored SSRC of current arbitrator, the floor participant: shall request the MCPTT client to render the received RTP media packets; shall clear the stored SSRC of the candidate arbitrator, if any; shall restart timer T203 (End of RTP media); and shall remain in 'O: has no permission' state. Otherwise, if SSRC of floor participant sending the media packet matches with the stored SSRC of candidate arbitrator, the floor participant: shall request the MCPTT client to render the received RTP media packets; shall set the stored SSRC of the current arbitrator to the SSRC of RTP media packet; shall clear the stored SSRC of the candidate arbitrator; shall restart timer T203 (End of RTP media); and shall remain in 'O: has no permission' state.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'O: has permission' > General
Content: In this state the MCPTT client is acting as a floor control server (floor arbitrator) and has the permission to send media. Timer T206 (Stop talking warning) and timer T207 (Stop Talking) are running in this state.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'O: has permission' > Send RTP Media packets (S: RTP Media)
Content: Upon receiving encoded media from the user or if encoded media is already buffered the floor participant: shall start timer T206 (Stop talking warning), if not running; shall request the MCPTT client to start sending RTP media packets towards other MCPTT clients; and shall remain in 'O: has permission' state.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'O: has permission' > Receive Floor Release message (R: Floor Release)
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Release message and if the value of "/ x / x /OffNetwork/QueueUsage" leaf node present in the group configuration as specified in 3GPP TS 24.483 is set to "true", the floor participant: shall remove the sender of the Floor Release message from the queue, if the User ID value in the floor release message matches the User ID value of the queued request; and shall remain in 'O: has permission' state.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'O: has permission' > Receive Floor Request message (R: Floor Request)
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Request message which is not pre-emptive as determined by clause 4.1.1.5, in a session where: the value of "/ x / x /OffNetwork/QueueUsage" leaf node present in the group configuration as specified in 3GPP TS 24.483 is set to "false"; or the value of "/ x / x /OffNetwork/QueueUsage" leaf node present in the group configuration as specified in 3GPP TS 24.483 is set to "true" but the F-bit in the Floor Indicator field is set to '0' (i.e. indicating that queueing of floor requests is not supported) or the Floor Indicator field is not included in the Floor Request message; then the floor participant: shall send the Floor Deny message. The Floor Deny message: shall include in the Reject Cause field the Reject Cause value cause \#1 (Another MCPTT client has permission); may include in the Reject Cause field an additional text string explaining the reason for rejecting the floor request in the Reject Phrase value; and shall include the User ID field received in the Floor Request message; and shall remain in 'O: has permission' state. Upon receiving a Floor Request message which is not pre-emptive as determined by clause 4.1.1.5, and the value of "/ x / x /OffNetwork/QueueUsage" leaf node present in the group configuration as specified in 3GPP TS 24.483 is set to "true" and the F-bit in the Floor Indicator field is set to '1' (i.e. indicating that queueing of the floor requests is supported) in the Floor Request message, the floor participant: if the Floor Request message is not already stored, then shall store the received Floor Request message; if the pending request queue is not full, shall send the Floor Queue Position Info message. The Floor Queue Position Info message: shall include in the User ID field the MCPTT ID of the floor participant sending the Floor Request message; shall include the SSRC of the floor participant in the SSRC of queued floor participant field; shall include the position in the floor request queue in the Queue Info field; and shall include the floor priority in the Queue Info field; if the pending request queue is full, shall send the Floor Deny message. The Floor Deny message: shall include in the Reject Cause field the Reject Cause value cause \#7 (Queue full); may include in the Reject Cause field an additional text string explaining the reason for rejecting the floor request in the Reject Phrase value; and shall include the User ID field received in the Floor Request message; and shall remain in 'O: has permission' state.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'O: has permission' > Send Floor Release message (PTT button released with no pending request in queue)
Content: Upon receiving an indication from the MCPTT user to release permission to send RTP media, the floor participant: shall stop timer T206 (Stop talking warning), if running; shall stop timer T207 (Stop talking), if running; shall send a Floor Release message towards other floor participants, if no queued requests exist: The Floor Release message: shall include the MCPTT ID of the MCPTT user in the User ID field; and if the session is not initiated as a broadcast group call with the B-bit set to '1' (Broadcast group call), shall include a Floor Indicator field set to '0' (normal call); shall start timer T230 (Inactivity); shall clear the stored SSRC of the current floor arbitrator; and shall enter 'O: silence' state.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'O: has permission' > Send Floor Granted message (PTT button released with pending request(s) in queue)
Content: When no more encoded media is received from the user and if at least one Floor Request message is stored (i.e. queueing mode is used in the session), the floor participant: shall stop timer T206 (Stop talking warning), if running; shall stop timer T207 (Stop talking), if running; shall request the MCPTT client to stop sending RTP media packets towards other MCPTT clients; shall send the Floor Granted message toward the other floor participants. The Floor Granted message: shall include the MCPTT ID of the first floor participant in the queue in the User ID field; shall include the SSRC of the first floor participant in the queue in the SSRC of the granted floor participant field; shall remove the first floor participant from the queue; for the remaining floor participants in the queue: shall include the MCPTT ID of the floor participant in the Queued User ID field; shall include the SSRC of the floor participant in the SSRC of queued floor participant field; shall include the queue position of the floor participant in the Queue Info field; and shall include the priority of the floor participant in the Queue Info field; and if the floor request is a broadcast group call, system call, emergency call or an imminent peril call, shall include a Floor Indicator field indicating the relevant call types; shall set the stored SSRC of the current floor arbitrator to the SSRC of user to whom the floor was granted in the Floor Granted message; shall start timer T205 (Floor Granted ) and shall initiate counter C205 (Floor Granted ) to 1; and shall enter the 'O: pending granted' state.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'O: has permission' > Receive Floor Request message with pre-emption indication (R: Floor Request with pre-emption)
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Request message which is pre-emptive as determined by clause 4.1.1.5, the floor participant: shall stop timer T206 (Stop talking warning), if running; shall stop timer T207 (Stop talking), if running; shall request the MCPTT client to stop sending RTP media packets towards other MCPTT clients; shall send a Floor Granted message towards the other floor participants. The Floor Granted message: shall include the MCPTT ID of the Floor Request message received in the User ID field; shall include the SSRC of floor participant sending the Floor Request message in the SSRC of floor control server field; and if the value of "/ x / x /OffNetwork/QueueUsage" leaf node present in the group configuration as specified in 3GPP TS 24.483 is set to "true", for each floor participant in the queue: shall include the MCPTT ID of the floor participant in the Queued User ID field; shall include the SSRC of the floor participant in the SSRC of queued floor participant field; shall include the queue position of the floor participant in the Queue Info field; and shall include the priority of the floor participant in the Queue Info field; shall start timer T205 (Floor Granted) and shall initiate counter C205 (Floor Granted) to 1; shall set the stored SSRC of the current floor arbitrator to the SSRC of the user to whom the floor was granted in the Floor Granted message; and shall enter the 'O: pending granted' state.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'O: has permission' > Receive Floor Queue Position Request message (R: Floor Queue Position Request)
Content: Upon receiving a Floor Queue Position Request message, the floor participant: shall send the Floor Queue Position Info message. The Floor Queue Position Info message: shall include the floor participant's own MCPTT ID in the User ID field; shall include the queue position and floor priority in the Queue Info field; shall include the value of the SSRC of floor participant requesting floor queue status info field from the received Floor Queue Position Request message in the SSRC of queued floor participant field; and shall include the value of the User ID field from the received Floor Queue Position Request message in the Queued User ID field; and remain in the 'O: has permission' state.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'O: has permission' > Transmission time limit warning (Timer T206 expires)
Content: When timer T206 (Stop talking warning) expires, the floor participant: may notify the MCPTT user that the transmission time limit is about to reach; shall start timer T207 (Stop talking); and shall remain in the current state.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'O: has permission' > Transmission time limit reached with pending request(s) in queue (Timer T207 expires)
Content: When the timer T207 (Stop talking) expires and if at least one Floor Request message is stored (i.e. queueing mode is used in the session), the floor participant: shall request the MCPTT client to stop sending RTP media packets towards other MCPTT clients; shall send the Floor Granted message toward the other floor participants. The Floor Granted message: a. shall include the MCPTT ID of the first floor participant in the queue in the User ID field; b. shall include the SSRC of the first floor participant in the queue in the SSRC of the granted floor participant field; c. shall remove the first floor participant from the queue; d. for the remaining floor participants in the queue: shall include the MCPTT ID of the floor participant in the Queued User ID field; shall include the SSRC of the floor participant in the SSRC of queued floor participant field; shall include the queue position of the floor participant in the Queue Info field; and shall include the priority of the floor participant in the Queue Info field; and e. if the floor request is a broadcast group call, system call, emergency call or an imminent peril call, shall include a Floor Indicator field indicating the relevant call types; shall set the stored SSRC of the current floor arbitrator to the SSRC of user to whom the floor was granted in the Floor Granted message; shall start timer T205 (Floor Granted ) and shall initiate counter C205 (Floor Granted ) to 1; and shall enter the 'O: pending granted' state.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'O: has permission' > Transmission time limit reached with no pending request in queue (Timer T207 expires)
Content: When timer T207 (Stop talking) expires with no pending requests in the queue or if the value of "/ x / x /OffNetwork/QueueUsage" leaf node present in the group configuration as specified in 3GPP TS 24.483 is set to "false", the floor participant: shall send a Floor Release message towards other floor participants. The Floor Release message: shall include the MCPTT ID of the MCPTT user in the User ID field; and if the session is not initiated as a broadcast group call with the B-bit set to '1' (Broadcast group call), shall include a Floor Indicator field set to '0' (normal call); shall start timer T230 (Inactivity); shall clear the stored SSRC of the current floor arbitrator; and shall enter 'O: silence' state.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'O: pending request' > General
Content: In this state the MCPTT client is waiting for a response to a Floor request message. In this state timer T201 (Floor Request) is running. To resolve race condition between multiple simultaneous floor requests, the MCPTT client resets the counter associated with timer T201, if another floor request with higher priority or higher SSRC, in case the priority is same, is received.
Title Path: Off-network floor control > Floor participant procedures > Floor participant state diagram basic operation > State: 'O: pending request' > Receive RTP media (R: RTP media)
Content: Upon receiving RTP media packets and if there is no stored SSRC of the current floor arbitrator, the floor participant: shall request the MCPTT client to render the RTP media packets; shall reset the value of the counter C201 (Floor Request) to 1; shall set the stored SSRC of the current floor arbitrator to the SSRC of RTP media packet; shall restart (or start, if not running already) timer T203 (End of RTP media); and shall remain in 'O: pending request' state. Otherwise, if SSRC of floor participant sending the media matches with the stored SSRC of current floor arbitrator, the floor participant: shall request the MCPTT client to render the RTP media packets; shall reset the value of the counter C201 (Floor Request) to 1; shall restart (or start, if not running already) timer T203 (End of RTP media); and shall remain in 'O: pending request' state. Otherwise, if SSRC of floor participant sending the media packet matches with the stored SSRC of candidate arbitrator, the floor participant: shall request the MCPTT client to render the RTP media packets; shall reset the value of the counter C201 (Floor Request) to 1; shall set the stored SSRC of the current arbitrator to the SSRC of RTP media packet; shall clear the stored SSRC of the candidate arbitrator; shall restart (or start, if not running already) timer T203 (End of RTP media); and shall remain in 'O: pending request' state.
